[1mdiff --git a/docs/gpu-driven-api-design.md b/docs/gpu-driven-api-design.md[m

[36m@@ -0,0 +1,1219 @@[m
[32m+[m[32m# GPU-driven renderer: final API design[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mStatus: design only. Nothing here is implemented. No migration phases — this is[m
[32m+[m[32mthe target, and either it is the right target or it isn't.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mScope: compare `mu_gfx` against the two upstream machinery snapshots in-tree[m
[32m+[m[32m(`fukuna_engine/`, `threedgame/`), then specify the refined public API, the[m
[32m+[m[32mGPU-side data layouts, the sync model and the bandwidth budget that make the[m
[32m+[m[32mrenderer both faster and more flexible than either.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mNumbers marked *(measured)* were printed by compiling the real headers[m
[32m+[m[32m(`sizeof`) or taken from a run log. Numbers marked *(model)* are arithmetic from[m
[32m+[m[32mthose counts and are labelled with the assumption.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 0. How the three compare, in mechanisms[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mEverything below is about *who owns visibility and command generation*, because[m
[32m+[m[32mthat is the only axis that separates a GPU-driven renderer from a CPU-driven one[m
[32m+[m[32mwith an indirect draw bolted on.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 0.1 mu_gfx (this repo) — GPU owns visibility[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m[32mgame writes SceneInstanceSource[]            (main.c, CPU)[m
[32m+[m[32m  -> CPU compacts: SceneGpuInstance[] (80B) + SceneCandidate[] (8B)   scene3d.c[m
[32m+[m[32m  -> upload (frame cmd buffer + staging ring, no submit/wait)[m
[32m+[m[32m  -> cull compute: frustum (6 dot) + Hi-Z occlusion    writes draws[].instanceCount[m
[32m+[m[32m                                                       writes visible[][m
[32m+[m[32m  -> one vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirect per mesh slot, index buffer bound[m
[32m+[m[32m     (pre-Count snapshot; steady state is one Count draw per view, 9.1)[m
[32m+[m[32m  VS: vertices pulled from a device address, rows from the instance record[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mFacts:[m
[32m+[m[32m- `ScenePush` is 248 bytes and near the 256-byte limit; Hi-Z params are already[m
[32m+[m[32m  bit-packed into the two former pad words (`src/scene3d_shared.h`).[m
[32m+[m[32m- `SceneDraw` is 32 bytes of which 12 are pad; the first 20 are exactly[m
[32m+[m[32m  `VkDrawIndexedIndirectCommand`. Stride is 32, not 20.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Per-draw work: `vkCmdBindIndexBuffer` + a 248-byte push + one indirect draw,[m
[32m+[m[32m  once per mesh slot (6 in the cubepets demo).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Skinning is designed but not wired**: `SceneSkinJob` (40 B, comment says 56)[m
[32m+[m[32m  and the `skin_input`/`skinned_vertices`/`palette` buffers exist,[m
[32m+[m[32m  `skin_job_count` is always 0, and there is no skinning pipeline in[m
[32m+[m[32m  `scene3d_init`. `SCENE_ASSET_SKINNED` is never consumed by a dispatch.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Doc drift already present: `SceneGpuMaterial` comment claims 112 bytes, it is[m
[32m+[m[32m  32; `SceneSkinJob` comment claims 56, it is 40. Both should be[m
[32m+[m[32m  `_Static_assert(sizeof(...) == N)` instead of prose.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mPer-frame bytes for 4096 instances / 6 mesh slots *(measured)*:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m| stream | bytes/frame | MB/s @60 |[m
[32m+[m[32m|---|---:|---:|[m
[32m+[m[32m| `SceneGpuInstance[]` upload | 327,680 | 19.7 |[m
[32m+[m[32m| `SceneCandidate[]` upload | 196,608 | 11.8 |[m
[32m+[m[32m| `SceneDraw[]` upload | 192 | 0.01 |[m
[32m+[m[32m| push data (1 cull + 6 draws) | 1,736 | 0.1 |[m
[32m+[m[32m| **total** | **526,216** | **31.6** |[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 0.2 fukuna_engine — CPU-built MDI, GPU vertex pull, blocking upload[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `GpuInstance` is 96 bytes: `mat4 model` + `vec4 bounds` + 4×u32[m
[32m+[m[32m  (`renderer_gpu_driven.h:17`).[m
[32m+[m[32m- The CPU builds one `VkDrawIndirectCommand` **per (instance, mesh)** with[m
[32m+[m[32m  `instanceCount = 1` and `firstInstance = i`, then uploads the array and issues a[m
[32m+[m[32m  single `vkCmdDrawIndirect` (`main.c:2734`, `main.c:2803`).[m
[32m+[m[32m- Draws are **non-indexed**: `vertexCount = mesh->lods[0].index_count`, and the[m
[32m+[m[32m  shader fetches indices by hand. `docs/GPU_DRIVEN_RENDERING_PLAN.md` §"Correct[m
[32m+[m[32m  indirect command type" says this is a known limitation.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The per-frame upload is a **full GPU stall**: `vk_end_one_time_cmd` =[m
[32m+[m[32m  `vkQueueSubmit` + `vkQueueWaitIdle` + `vkFreeCommandBuffers`[m
[32m+[m[32m  (`helpers.h:227`), called every frame from `rendering_system_upload_frame`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- `renderer_gpu_driven.c:77` — `rendering_system_record()` is `(void)`-casts and[m
[32m+[m[32m  a comment: *"CPU draw path remains primary until compute path implemented"*.[m
[32m+[m[32m- No cull / compaction / LOD compute shader exists in `shaders/`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Their own plan document lists the endpoint (compute cull → compute command[m
[32m+[m[32m  generation → `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount`) as **future work**.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 0.3 threedgame — per-mesh draws, one instance each, 128-byte draw records[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `GltfIndirectDrawData` is 128 bytes **per mesh**: 4 device addresses[m
[32m+[m[32m  (vertex/index/joints/weights) + `mat4 model` + material id + skin offset +[m
[32m+[m[32m  flags + `bloom_color[3]`. Uploaded **every frame**[m
[32m+[m[32m  (`main.c:35`, filled at `main.c:2253`, uploaded at `main.c:2274`).[m
[32m+[m[32m- `instanceCount = 1`, `firstInstance = i` (the *mesh* index) — one draw per[m
[32m+[m[32m  mesh, and the instance transform is baked into the mesh's draw record[m
[32m+[m[32m  (`main.c:1541`). Instancing is not used at all on this path.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Draw count is CPU-written: `uint32_t draw_count = mesh_count` is uploaded each[m
[32m+[m[32m  frame and consumed by `vkCmdDrawIndirectCount` (`main.c:2126`, `main.c:2314`).[m
[32m+[m[32m- Vertices are packed to 16 bytes (`PackedVertex`), normals 10-10-10 + bitangent[m
[32m+[m[32m  sign, tangent 8+8, uv half2 — the same budget as our `ScenePackedVertex`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Adds real renderer features we do not have: bloom, DoF, toon outline, analytic[m
[32m+[m[32m  fog, a glTF loader with material texturing, audio, and a filesystem layer.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Also has **no cull compute**: `grep -rl cull */shaders/` returns nothing in[m
[32m+[m[32m  either upstream tree.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 1. Mechanism-by-mechanism table[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m| | mu_gfx | fukuna_engine | threedgame |[m
[32m+[m[32m|---|---|---|---|[m
[32m+[m[32m| instance record | 80 B (`rows[3]`, bounds, tint) | 96 B (mat4, bounds, 4 ids) | none (transform inside the per-mesh draw record) |[m
[32m+[m[32m| bounds | stored per instance | stored per instance | absent (no culling) |[m
[32m+[m[32m| command generation | GPU (cull compute) | CPU array, uploaded | CPU array, uploaded |[m
[32m+[m[32m| command stride | 32 B (`VkDrawIndexedIndirectCommand` + 12 pad) | 16 B, one per (instance,mesh) | 16 B, one per mesh |[m
[32m+[m[32m| instances per draw | N (GPU-written `instanceCount`) | 1 | 1 |[m
[32m+[m[32m| draw count | static per mesh slot, CPU-known | CPU-computed | CPU-written to a GPU buffer |[m
[32m+[m[32m| indexed? | yes, index buffer bound, uint16 | no, manual index fetch | yes, via device address |[m
[32m+[m[32m| vertex fetch | device address, pulled in VS | device address, pulls indices by hand | device address, pulled in VS |[m
[32m+[m[32m| upload sync | frame cmd buffer + staging ring | `vkQueueWaitIdle` **per frame** | frame cmd buffer + staging ring |[m
[32m+[m[32m| occlusion culling | Hi-Z, compute, previous-frame depth | none | none |[m
[32m+[m[32m| LOD | none | none | none |[m
[32m+[m[32m| skinning | designed, not wired | compute kernel exists | CPU-side per-mesh transforms |[m
[32m+[m[32m| 2D | own stage + GPU sprite cull | sprite pipeline only | sprite pipeline only |[m
[32m+[m[32m| pass API | `begin_pass(PassDesc)` resolves layouts and stages | raw `vkCmdBeginRendering` at call sites | raw `vkCmdBeginRendering` at call sites |[m
[32m+[m[32m| image state | tracker per mip, `rt_transition_*` | tracker per mip (upstream of ours) | same |[m
[32m+[m[32m| buffer state | none - hand-written `cmd_buffer_barrier` | none | none |[m
[32m+[m[32m| deferred destroy | timeline `DeleteQueue` | none | none |[m
[32m+[m[32m| memory accounting | per-tag VMA tracker + profiler panel | none | none |[m
[32m+[m[32m| pipeline hot reload | `pipeline_mark_dirty` / `pipeline_rebuild` | same | same |[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe single structural difference that matters: **in `mu_gfx` the CPU never learns[m
[32m+[m[32mthe visible count and never builds a command**, and in both upstream snapshots it[m
[32m+[m[32mdoes both, every frame.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 1.1 What the upstream snapshots do better, and what is worth taking[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m1. **The `PUSH_CONSTANT(name, BODY)` macro** (`threedgame/vk.h:1237`) declares a[m
[32m+[m[32m   struct padded to exactly 256 bytes plus an unpadded `name##_init` for the C[m
[32m+[m[32m   initializer, so `vkCmdPushConstants` always transfers 256 bytes. We[m
[32m+[m[32m   deliberately do the opposite (push `sizeof(struct)`, currently 248) - cheaper,[m
[32m+[m[32m   but it is why we ran out of push space for Hi-Z. Take the *idea* (a[m
[32m+[m[32m   compile-time size assertion per push struct) and reject the padding.[m
[32m+[m[32m2. **Per-draw device addresses for skinning inputs** (`joints_ptr`, `weights_ptr`[m
[32m+[m[32m   in the draw record). If skinning becomes real, those belong in the *mesh*[m
[32m+[m[32m   table, not in a per-instance record.[m
[32m+[m[32m3. **Feature breadth**: bloom, DoF, toon, fog, and `FSOutput` writing toon data to[m
[32m+[m[32m   a second target (`threedgame/shaders/gltf_uber.slang`). Not architectural, but[m
[32m+[m[32m   the API must leave room for extra render targets and per-pass parameters[m
[32m+[m[32m   without a rewrite. See 3.7.[m
[32m+[m[32m4. **Non-indexed indirect draws with manual index reads** (fukuna) is a trap, not[m
[32m+[m[32m   a trick: it doubles vertex-fetch traffic for any mesh whose indices repeat. Do[m
[32m+[m[32m   not copy it.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 1.2 What `mu_gfx` is behind on[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Metadata is not the bottleneck.** At 4096 instances x ~6.2 k vertices x 16 B[m
[32m+[m[32m  the vertex stream alone is ~408 MB/frame *(model: 4096 x 6219 x 16)*, ~24 GB/s at[m
[32m+[m[32m  60 fps, against 0.53 MB of per-frame metadata. Index reads add ~177 MB/frame at[m
[32m+[m[32m  uint16 *(model)*. Improving occlusion culling and adding LOD is worth 10-50x[m
[32m+[m[32m  more than any instance-record compaction. Compaction is still worth doing[m
[32m+[m[32m  (it removes CPU work) but it is not the headline.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **No LOD, no clusters/meshlets, no multi-view.** Shadows, reflection probes and[m
[32m+[m[32m  cube faces all need a second cull with different constants.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **One 248-byte push layout serves cull, VS and FS**, so the VS pays for 96 bytes[m
[32m+[m[32m  of frustum planes it cannot use and the cull kernel pays for material fields it[m
[32m+[m[32m  cannot use.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Per-draw push and per-draw index-buffer bind** instead of one push per pass[m
[32m+[m[32m  and zero per draw.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **The GPU-driven path is not measurable.** No counter for frustum-culled vs[m
[32m+[m[32m  Hi-Z-culled vs drawn, and no buffer readback in the backend (`vk.h` has[m
[32m+[m[32m  `texture_readback` only). Culling effectiveness is currently unfalsifiable from[m
[32m+[m[32m  inside the program.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Two stale sizeof comments** (0.1) prove prose in headers rots; the refined[m
[32m+[m[32m  headers must assert sizes instead.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **`SceneInstanceSource` is 52 bytes and read every frame** (4096 x 52 = 213 KB of[m
[32m+[m[32m  CPU-side traffic plus a pointer chase into the asset) to produce 80 bytes per[m
[32m+[m[32m  instance for the GPU.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 2. Where the bytes actually go[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mModel for the cubepets scene: 4096 instances, 6 mesh slots, ~6.2 k vertices and[m
[32m+[m[32m~21.6 k indices per model, 60 fps, everything submitted before culling.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m| traffic | per frame | at 60 fps | note |[m
[32m+[m[32m|---|---:|---:|---|[m
[32m+[m[32m| vertex stream reads (VS) | ~408 MB | 24.5 GB/s | 4096 x 6219 x 16 B, shrinks with culling and LOD |[m
[32m+[m[32m| index stream reads | ~177 MB | 10.6 GB/s | uint16, shrinks with culling and LOD |[m
[32m+[m[32m| instance upload | 328 KB | 20 MB/s | 80 B x 4096, every frame |[m
[32m+[m[32m| candidate upload | 197 KB | 12 MB/s | 8 B x 24576 |[m
[32m+[m[32m| push + commands | ~2 KB | 0.1 MB/s | |[m
[32m+[m[32m| **total** | **~585 MB** | **~35 GB/s** | order of magnitude, not a measurement |[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mRanked conclusions, which is what the design follows:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m1. **Vertex and index traffic dominate by three orders of magnitude.** The API[m
[32m+[m[32m   must make LOD/cluster selection and occlusion culling first-class, not extras.[m
[32m+[m[32m2. **The 328 KB/frame instance upload is not a bandwidth problem, it is a CPU[m
[32m+[m[32m   problem.** The CPU has to *author* 4096 x 80 B records per frame: transform,[m
[32m+[m[32m   bounds, candidate mirror, memcpy. Compaction buys CPU time and cache footprint,[m
[32m+[m[32m   and it is the prerequisite for dirty-only uploads.[m
[32m+[m[32m3. **Anything read once per candidate is cheap** (the cull kernel reads 327 KB of[m
[32m+[m[32m   instances + 197 KB of candidates), so ALU-vs-bytes trades inside the cull[m
[32m+[m[32m   kernel should always resolve toward fewer bytes.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 3. Final design[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.1 Three lifetimes, three data sets[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe whole design follows from splitting state by lifetime instead of by "which[m
[32m+[m[32mpass reads it".[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m| lifetime | owner | contents | upload |[m
[32m+[m[32m|---|---|---|---|[m
[32m+[m[32m| **Asset** (immutable) | `SceneAssets` | mesh table, vertex streams, index streams, materials, local bounds, LOD/cluster tables | once, at load |[m
[32m+[m[32m| **Scene** (persistent) | `SceneInstances` | instance records (quantized TRS + flags), slot allocator, dirty ranges | dirty slots only |[m
[32m+[m[32m| **View/Frame** (transient) | `SceneFrame` | per-view constants, visible lists, indirect commands, counters, Hi-Z chain | never (GPU writes; CPU only zeroes counters) |[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mConsequences that the current code cannot express:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- An instance that does not move costs **zero** upload bytes per frame.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The cull kernel reads *every* instance but only the compact form (§3.2), and the[m
[32m+[m[32m  VS reads the same compact form for visible ones — one array, two readers, no[m
[32m+[m[32m  duplicated bounds.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Nothing that is per-view lives in a per-instance or per-asset struct, so a[m
[32m+[m[32m  second view (shadow map) is another element of an array, not a new code path.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.2 GPU-side layouts (byte budgets, exact)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Instance — 20 bytes, replaces 80.** Transform is quantized and rebuilt in the[m
[32m+[m[32mshader; bounds are derived, not stored. **Decision made: quantized TRS (Q1/Q2,[m
[32m+[m[32m2026-09-27)** — half3 position + half uniform scale + 4 spare bytes +[m
[32m+[m[32m10-10-10-2 quaternion + 4 flag bytes.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* 20 bytes. 4-byte aligned (storage buffer rules); no 16-byte padding games. */[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct GpuInstance {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint16_t pos[3];    /* 6  half  chunk-relative position (Q1)            */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint16_t scale;     /* 2  half  uniform scale                         */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t spare;     /* 4  reserved (chunk id, future use)               */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t quat;      /* 4  10-10-10-2 normalized quaternion            */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint8_t  tint;      /* 1  palette index                               */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint8_t  flags;     /* 1  bit0 dynamic, bit1 skinned, bit2 shadow,    */[m
[32m+[m[32m                        /*    bit3 two-sided, bits4-7 spare               */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint8_t  lod_bias;  /* 1  signed bias added to the projected LOD      */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint8_t  mesh_set;  /* 1  mesh-set id, 256 sets (Q12)                 */[m
[32m+[m[32m} GpuInstance;          /* 20 bytes.                                       */[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Q3 resolved 2026-09-27: uniform per-instance scale. Source assets may[m
[32m+[m[32m   carry any node transform (TRS or matrix); the importer canonicalizes[m
[32m+[m[32m   them before they reach this record: non-uniform scale and shear are[m
[32m+[m[32m   baked into static mesh vertices at load, negative scale is resolved[m
[32m+[m[32m   in the loader (it flips winding/culling), and skinned meshes keep[m
[32m+[m[32m   their skeleton/skin space coherent with the instance transform held[m
[32m+[m[32m   separate. By the time the renderer sees it, every instance is[m
[32m+[m[32m   translation + rotation + uniform scale. The loader never refuses a[m
[32m+[m[32m   mesh for non-uniform scale; it bakes it. */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mBudget: 20 B vs 80 B is a 4x reduction, and it removes three float4s of[m
[32m+[m[32mstructural padding, world bounds, and the 4th implicit row. Reconstruction cost in[m
[32m+[m[32mthe VS and in the cull kernel is ~12 multiplies (quat to basis) plus 3 row dots;[m
[32m+[m[32mat 20 bytes per read against a bandwidth-bound kernel, ALU is the cheaper side[m
[32m+[m[32m(2).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mWhy **not** store `rows[3]` in half precision (24 B, no reconstruction): it is[m
[32m+[m[32monly 4 bytes more but it makes every consumer pay for 24 B, and it does not[m
[32m+[m[32manswer LOD-Hi-Z (which needs the world radius anyway). Shear is not a reason to[m
[32m+[m[32mkeep rows either: the importer bakes shear (and non-uniform scale) into static[m
[32m+[m[32mmesh vertices at load (Q3), so no per-instance representation needs it. Rows[m
[32m+[m[32mstay the fallback if profiling shows the quaternion reconstruction is hot -[m
[32m+[m[32msee Q2.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Mesh — 40 bytes, indexed by mesh slot.** Replaces the per-draw push: the VS gets[m
[32m+[m[32mthe mesh slot from the visible entry (§3.4), so no per-draw constant is needed.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct GpuMesh {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint64_t vertex_stream;  /* 8  device address into the pooled vertex stream  */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint64_t index_stream;   /* 8  device address into the pooled index stream   */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t index_count;    /* 4  this LOD's index count (or 0 if non-indexed)  */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t pack;           /* 4  base_vertex:20 | material:12                  */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint16_t first_cluster;  /* 2  cluster table entry (meshlet path, 3.5)       */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint16_t cluster_count;  /* 2                                                  */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint16_t flags;          /* 2  index width, skinned, double-sided, alpha     */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint16_t lod_group;      /* 2  LOD ladder this mesh belongs to               */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t local_center;   /* 4  half3 local bounding sphere centre            */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t local_radius;   /* 4  half radius + half spare                    */[m
[32m+[m[32m} GpuMesh;                   /* 40 bytes, ~64 mesh slots: 2.5 KB, L1 resident    */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe world sphere the cull kernel needs is `center_w = pos + basis * (scale *[m
[32m+[m[32mlocal_center)`, `radius_w = local_radius * scale` — exact under uniform[m
[32m+[m[32mscale (Q3), no max() needed. That is two extra ALU ops per candidate and[m
[32m+[m[32m**zero** bytes per instance, versus 16 B/instance stored today.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Material — 12 bytes, replaces 32.**[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct GpuMaterial {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t base_color; /* 4  UNORM8x4                                         */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t texture;    /* 4  bindless albedo TextureID:16 | normal/orm:16      */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t flags;      /* 4  sampler:2 | alpha_mode:2 | uv_set:1 | ...         */[m
[32m+[m[32m} GpuMaterial;           /* array in a device buffer; the FS reads it by id      */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe sampler becomes a 2-bit field because the renderer only ever wants[m
[32m+[m[32mlinear-clamp or nearest-clamp for scene materials; arbitrary samplers stay[m
[32m+[m[32mavailable through the bindless table for effects.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Draw — exactly 20 bytes (stride 20, a legal multiple of 4).** Today it is 32 with[m
[32m+[m[32m12 bytes of pad whose only purpose was alignment.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Identical to VkDrawIndexedIndirectCommand. Written by the GPU, never by the CPU. */[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct GpuDraw {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t index_count;    /* GPU-written: LOD selection (3.5)      */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t instance_count; /* GPU-written: the cull kernel          */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t first_index;    /* static: from the mesh table LOD row   */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t vertex_offset;  /* static: from the mesh table           */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t first_instance; /* GPU-written: base into visible[]      */[m
[32m+[m[32m} GpuDraw;[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m`first_index`, `vertex_offset` and `index_count` are constants for the (mesh, LOD)[m
[32m+[m[32mbucket and are filled **once** at asset load, not per frame. Only[m
[32m+[m[32m`instance_count` and `first_instance` change per frame, and only the GPU writes[m
[32m+[m[32mthem. Stride 20 forfeits 32-byte alignment of each record; with a handful of draw[m
[32m+[m[32mbuckets that is irrelevant, and it is 37% fewer bytes than today (Q8).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**View constants — 96 bytes per view, read through one push address.** This is what[m
[32m+[m[32mreplaces the 248-byte push layout.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct GpuView {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float    rows[4][4];   /* 64  view-projection, dot-able rows (as today)      */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint64_t draws;        /* 8   device address of this view's GpuDraw[]        */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint64_t visible;      /* 8   device address of this view's visible[]        */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t draw_count;   /* 4   buckets, CPU-known at load                     */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t caps;         /* 4   visible capacity, Hi-Z level count, flags      */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t viewport[2];  /* 8   for LOD projection and Hi-Z LOD selection      */[m
[32m+[m[32m} GpuView;                 /* per-view, not per-instance, not per-draw           */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Push — 24 bytes, once per pass, zero per draw:**[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct ViewPush {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint64_t view;   /* GpuView* for the cull dispatch / this draw set */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint64_t meshes; /* GpuMesh[] base address                         */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t sun;    /* packed half3 direction + half ambient          */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t misc;   /* Hi-Z base:16 | levels:8 | flags:8              */[m
[32m+[m[32m} ViewPush;[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mEverything else the shaders need (materials, hiz size, instance base, candidate[m
[32m+[m[32mbase) is reachable from `ViewPush.view` or is a `Load` on a small, L1-resident[m
[32m+[m[32mtable. The 96 bytes of frustum planes leave the push entirely: the cull kernel[m
[32m+[m[32mderives them from `rows[]` once per thread with 6 `length()` calls (3.5) or reads[m
[32m+[m[32mthem from a per-view extended block.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.3 The cull kernel (one kernel, N views)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mOne dispatch per view, `numthreads(64,1,1)`, one thread per candidate. The[m
[32m+[m[32mcandidate list itself changes shape:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Today**: the CPU expands each instance into one candidate per mesh[m
[32m+[m[32m  (`SceneCandidate`, 8 B, 24576 entries for the demo) and uploads them.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Target**: the CPU uploads nothing. The kernel thread is a *candidate*,[m
[32m+[m[32m  and the ids are derived from the dispatch id while mesh-set size stays[m
[32m+[m[32m  bounded (Q6): `iid = tid / MAX_MESHES_PER_SET`,[m
[32m+[m[32m  `local_m = tid % MAX_MESHES_PER_SET`. The mesh set id lives in the[m
[32m+[m[32m  instance's `mesh_set` byte (Q12), so the loop bound is a table lookup:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* pseudocode of the final cull thread (Q6: derived ids, bounded sets) */[m
[32m+[m[32muint iid     = tid.x / MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m[32muint local_m = tid.x % MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m[32mGpuInstance inst = instances[iid];                 /* one load, shared below */[m
[32m+[m[32muint set   = inst.mesh_set;                        /* Q12: full byte, 256 sets */[m
[32m+[m[32muint first = mesh_set_first[set], count = mesh_set_count[set];[m
[32m+[m[32mif (local_m >= count) return;                      /* tail lanes exit */[m
[32m+[m[32muint m = first + local_m;[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat3x3 basis = quat_to_basis(inst.quat);         /* ~12 mul */[m
[32m+[m[32mGpuMesh mesh = meshes[m];                          /* L1: 40 B, ~64 entries */[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat3 c; float r;[m
[32m+[m[32mworld_sphere(inst, basis, mesh, c, r);             /* derive, don't store */[m
[32m+[m[32mif (sphere_outside_frustum(c, r, rows)) return;    /* mirrored-plane fast path (Q5), 6-plane fallback */[m
[32m+[m[32mif (hiz_occluded(c, r, rows, viewport, hiz)) return; /* prev-frame Hi-Z default, 9.8 */ /* §0.1 of Hi-Z work */[m
[32m+[m[32muint slot;[m
[32m+[m[32mInterlockedAdd(draws[draw_index(m, lod)].instance_count, 1, slot);[m
[32m+[m[32mvisible[draws[..].first_instance + slot] = iid | (m << 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mTwo wins over the current shape:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m1. **No candidate array, no candidate upload, no CPU expansion.** Dispatch[m
[32m+[m[32m   `instance_count * MAX_MESHES_PER_SET` threads (Q6); each thread derives[m
[32m+[m[32m   its (instance, mesh) pair from its id, so candidate-level parallelism[m
[32m+[m[32m   is kept while the CPU writes nothing. Tail lanes (`local_m >= count`)[m
[32m+[m[32m   exit early. The per-instance transform is re-read per candidate thread[m
[32m+[m[32m   rather than shared across a loop; at 4096 instances x 6 meshes that is[m
[32m+[m[32m   24576 tests either way and total work is identical.[m
[32m+[m[32m2. **Draw index and LOD come from the mesh row**, so the write target is[m
[32m+[m[32m   computed on the GPU. (Pre-`Count` history: the CPU once knew the bucket[m
[32m+[m[32m   count statically as mesh_count x lod_count; §9 replaces buckets with[m
[32m+[m[32m   compacted commands and the `Count` draw.)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mFrustum test: mirrored-plane fast path for normal perspective cameras, generic[m
[32m+[m[32m6-plane fallback for shadow / off-center / orthographic views (Q5, §9.2).[m
[32m+[m[32mThe VS never reads either; the kernel derives the fast path from the[m
[32m+[m[32mprojection rows with no 96-byte plane block in the push.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.4 The draw path: zero per-draw state (superseded by §9; pre-`Count` history)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m> §9.1/§9.4 replace this section's one-draw-per-mesh-slot loop with one[m
[32m+[m[32m> `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount` per view over GPU-compacted commands. The[m
[32m+[m[32m> `visible[i] = instance | mesh << 16` packing and the draw loop below are the[m
[32m+[m[32m> initial shape (§9.1/§9.9), not a contract; the entry format stays[m
[32m+[m[32m> implementation-private (Q28).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe problem with "one push per pass" is that the VS no longer knows which mesh it[m
[32m+[m[32mis drawing. Solution: **pack the mesh slot into the visible entry**.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m[32mvisible[i] = instance_slot | (mesh_slot << 16)   /* 16 bits of instance, 16 of mesh */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- 16 bits of instance caps a view at 65535 *visible* instances, which is the same[m
[32m+[m[32m  order as today's `SCENE3D_MAX_INSTANCES`; if that is not enough, move to 20/12 or[m
[32m+[m[32m  make the visible entry 8 bytes (Q7).[m
[32m+[m[32m- The VS reads `visible[first_instance + SV_InstanceID]`, splits both halves, and[m
[32m+[m[32m  fetches `GpuMesh` (40 B, L1, and *uniform across the whole draw* so it is a[m
[32m+[m[32m  broadcast) to get `vertex_stream`, `material`, `index_count`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The FS receives `mesh_slot` as `nointerpolation uint` and fetches[m
[32m+[m[32m  `GpuMaterial` (12 B, also uniform) - exactly what `gltf_uber.slang` does with[m
[32m+[m[32m  `material_id` today, so this pattern is proven on this codebase family.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe resulting draw loop is:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* once per pass */[m
[32m+[m[32mbegin_pass(vk, cmd, &(PassDesc){ .colors = &color, .color_count = 1, .depth = &depth,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .pipeline = view_pipeline,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .push = BYTE_SPAN(view_push),[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .reads = buffers_cull_output, .read_count = N });[m
[32m+[m[32m/* once per view, not per draw */[m
[32m+[m[32mcmd_bind_index_buffer(cmd, view->index_stream, VK_INDEX_TYPE_UINT16);[m
[32m+[m[32mcmd_draw_indexed_indirect(cmd, draws_slice, view->draw_count, 20, /*push*/ NULL);[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mLosing the per-draw push also removes the per-draw `vkCmdBindIndexBuffer`: today it[m
[32m+[m[32mis bound per mesh slot because each model owns a separate index buffer. Pooling all[m
[32m+[m[32mindex data into one arena with a device-address per mesh (as `GpuMesh.index_stream`[m
[32m+[m[32mdoes) makes it one bind per *pass*, and the hardware reads indices from wherever[m
[32m+[m[32mthe draw's `first_index` lands. That is the same trick the vertex stream already[m
[32m+[m[32muses, applied to indices.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mNet per-frame CPU recording cost for the scene pass: **one pass begin + one index[m
[32m+[m[32mbind + one indirect draw**, against today's 6 x (bind + push + draw). The design[m
[32m+[m[32mgoal is that the number of CPU-side Vulkan calls per frame is a function of *passes[m
[32m+[m[32mand views*, not of geometry.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.5 LOD and clusters (where the real bandwidth is; static-bucket text superseded by §9.4-§9.5)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe Hi-Z work already computes `w_clip` and the projected NDC radius for every[m
[32m+[m[32mcandidate. That is the same quantity LOD selection needs:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m[32mscreen_px = radius_w * proj_scale / w_near + lod_bias   /* proj_scale = |row.xyz| */[m
[32m+[m[32mlod       = lod_ladder[screen_px]                        /* precomputed bucket table */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mSo LOD selection is **free** - it is arithmetic on data the occlusion test already[m
[32m+[m[32mproduced. What it needs from the API is a draw-bucket layout that lets the cull[m
[32m+[m[32mkernel write one instance into one of several LOD buckets (pre-Count shape; 9 replaces buckets with per-pair LOD in compacted commands):[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m[32mdraw bucket index = mesh_slot * LOD_COUNT + lod[m
[32m+[m[32mGpuDraw[view][mesh_slot * LOD_COUNT + lod]   /* all buckets exist, most stay empty */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- Bucket count is `mesh_count x LOD_COUNT`, known at load, so the CPU still issues a (pre-Count; superseded by 9.4 — static buckets are gone)[m
[32m+[m[32m  single `draw_indexed_indirect` with a static count. Empty buckets cost one[m
[32m+[m[32m  indirect draw record read by the hardware, which is ~nothing (20 B each).[m
[32m+[m[32m- `index_count`, `first_index`, `vertex_offset` for each (mesh, LOD) are static and[m
[32m+[m[32m  written once at load.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The vertex stream must be LOD-aware: either one stream per LOD ladder (each[m
[32m+[m[32m  `GpuMesh` row points into it) or one stream with LOD ranges. The pool already[m
[32m+[m[32m  supports either.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **This is the 4-8x knob.** At 16 B/vertex and 4096 instances, dropping distant[m
[32m+[m[32m  instances to LOD2 (25% of the indices) removes ~280 MB/frame on its own.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mCluster/meshlet path, which composes with the above rather than replacing it:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `GpuMesh.first_cluster` / `cluster_count` point into a cluster table (64-128 B per[m
[32m+[m[32m  cluster: bounding sphere, cone axis + cutoff, index offset/count or a 64-bit mask).[m
[32m+[m[32m- The instance-level cull stays as designed. A **second** pass per visible instance[m
[32m+[m[32m  tests its clusters, writing `(instance | mesh << 16, cluster)` entries into the[m
[32m+[m[32m  same draw bucket, so the draw path does not change shape at all - the visible[m
[32m+[m[32m  array simply holds cluster-draws instead of instance-draws.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Cone culling kills back-facing clusters before the vertex stage; on the cubepets[m
[32m+[m[32m  crowd this is the difference between "24 GB/s of vertex fetch" and maybe a third[m
[32m+[m[32m  of that.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Decision needed on whether the *asset format* grows cluster tables now (Q10), since[m
[32m+[m[32m  it changes the loader and the .glb pipeline, not just the renderer.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.6 Sync model: buffers belong in the pass description[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mToday the cull-to-draw dependency is hand-written at the call site[m
[32m+[m[32m(`scene3d.c`: two `cmd_buffer_barrier` calls naming `COMPUTE`,[m
[32m+[m[32m`SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE`, `DRAW_INDIRECT`, `VERTEX_SHADER` and access masks). That is[m
[32m+[m[32mexactly the knowledge the pass API exists to own, and the doctrine says call sites[m
[32m+[m[32mname intent, never stage masks.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mProposed extension (the only `vk.h` change the renderer needs):[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Coarse per-buffer state. One state covers the whole Buffer; regions are not[m
[32m+[m[32m   tracked because the pools are few and the barrier cost is a dependency, not a[m
[32m+[m[32m   flush. If that proves wrong, this grows a (offset,size) range per state. */[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct BufferAccess {[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice           slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkPipelineStageFlags2 stage;[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkAccessFlags2        access;[m
[32m+[m[32m} BufferAccess;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct PassDesc {[m
[32m+[m[32m    const PassAttachment *colors;   uint32_t color_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const PassAttachment *depth;[m
[32m+[m[32m    RenderTarget *const  *shader_reads;  uint32_t shader_read_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    RenderTarget *const  *shader_writes; uint32_t shader_write_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const BufferAccess   *buf_reads;     uint32_t buf_read_count;   /* NEW */[m
[32m+[m[32m    const BufferAccess   *buf_writes;    uint32_t buf_write_count;  /* NEW */[m
[32m+[m[32m    ByteSpan              push;                                     /* NEW */[m
[32m+[m[32m    PipelineID            pipeline;[m
[32m+[m[32m} PassDesc;[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `buf_writes` gets `COMPUTE | SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE` (or `TRANSFER`), `buf_reads`[m
[32m+[m[32m  gets whatever the consumer needs (`DRAW_INDIRECT`, `VERTEX_SHADER`). `begin_pass`[m
[32m+[m[32m  flushes the resulting barriers *before* the pass begins, which is exactly the[m
[32m+[m[32m  point in the frame where they are needed.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The compute-only path (`color_count == 0`) already exists in `begin_pass`; a cull[m
[32m+[m[32m  dispatch becomes a pass with `buf_reads = {instances, meshes}`, `buf_writes =[m
[32m+[m[32m  {draws, visible, counters}` and no attachments. No `vkCmdBeginRendering`, no[m
[32m+[m[32m  `end_pass`, one flush.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Keep the per-mip `ImageState` tracker and the timeline `DeleteQueue`; both are[m
[32m+[m[32m  right. Add one rule that the current code violates implicitly: **frame-lifetime[m
[32m+[m[32m  resources (Hi-Z chain, visible arrays, draw arrays) are created at init/resize and[m
[32m+[m[32m  destroyed only at shutdown**, never per frame, so `wait_idle` never appears inside[m
[32m+[m[32m  the frame loop (the Hi-Z resize path currently calls `wait_idle` mid-frame, which[m
[32m+[m[32m  is correct but is a symptom of lazily-sized resources).[m
[32m+[m[32m- Barrier count per frame becomes: 1 for uploads, 1 per cull pass, 1 per graphics[m
[32m+[m[32m  pass, 1 for Hi-Z level chain. Today it is uploads + 2 hand-written + 7 Hi-Z level[m
[32m+[m[32m  flushes.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.7 Room for views, targets and effects[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `GpuView[]` is an array with a count, so a shadow cascade, a reflection pass or a[m
[32m+[m[32m  cube face is one more entry. Each view owns its own draws/visible/Hi-Z state; the[m
[32m+[m[32m  cull dispatch runs once per view with the same pipeline.[m
[32m+[m[32m- `PassDesc.push` means an effect pass (bloom, DoF, toon, fog) carries its own[m
[32m+[m[32m  parameter block without a second push layout, and `shader_writes` already models[m
[32m+[m[32m  storage-image post chains (`SINGLE_COMPUTE_POSTPROCESS.md` in the upstream tree is[m
[32m+[m[32m  the same idea, implemented there with a hand-written push struct per pass).[m
[32m+[m[32m- MRT: `GraphicsPipelineConfig` already takes `color_attachment_count` and a format[m
[32m+[m[32m  array; `threedgame`'s `FSOutput` (color + toon data) maps onto it with no API[m
[32m+[m[32m  change.[m
[32m+[m[32m- If TAA or motion vectors ever land, they come from a sidecar/history buffer,[m
[32m+[m[32m  never from growing the instance record — §3.2's 20-byte budget stands.[m
[32m+[m[32m  See Q11.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.8 Upload model: dirty slots, not whole scenes[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* 24 bytes: a slot index and its new record. The game owns the dirty list. */[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct InstanceUpdate {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t    slot;[m
[32m+[m[32m    GpuInstance data;   /* 20 B */[m
[32m+[m[32m} InstanceUpdate;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* One staging copy for the whole dirty set; no per-slot submission. */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_upload_instances(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd,[m
[32m+[m[32m                            Span(const InstanceUpdate) updates);[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mRules:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- The instance array is a **persistent device buffer**, allocated once at[m
[32m+[m[32m  `scene_create(capacity)`; it is never re-uploaded wholesale.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The game writes only what moved. A spinning crowd where every pet rotates means[m
[32m+[m[32m  4096 updates (98 KB) instead of 4096 x 80 B (328 KB) - same order, but the CPU[m
[32m+[m[32m  cost per update drops from "transform + bounds + candidate mirror" to "quantize[m
[32m+[m[32m  TRS".[m
[32m+[m[32m- Static geometry uploads once at spawn: 0 bytes/frame.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Pose dedupe for skinning**: if skinning is wired up, `SceneSkinJob` should be[m
[32m+[m[32m  deduplicated by `(model, clip, quantized_time)` so N characters sharing one[m
[32m+[m[32m  animation frame share one palette. 4096 palettes x 60 joints x 64 B would be[m
[32m+[m[32m  15 MB/frame; one unique pose is 3.8 KB. The dedupe table is a CPU hash; the GPU[m
[32m+[m[32m  side just reads `palette_addr` from the instance's skin record. This is a bigger[m
[32m+[m[32m  win than anything else in this document *if the demo ever becomes skinned* (Q13).[m
[32m+[m[32m- Uploads go through the frame command buffer and the existing staging ring. There[m
[32m+[m[32m  is no `vkQueueSubmit`/`vkQueueWaitIdle` anywhere in the frame loop - that is the[m
[32m+[m[32m  single worst thing in `fukuna_engine`'s frame (`helpers.h:227`).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 3.9 Instrumentation as part of the API, not an afterthought[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe doctrine is measure -> identify -> change -> measure, and right now the[m
[32m+[m[32mrenderer cannot be measured from inside. The final API must include:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct SceneCounters {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t submitted;      /* instances in the array                  */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t culled_frustum;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t culled_hiz;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t drawn;          /* instances actually in a draw            */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t lod[4];         /* instances per LOD bucket                */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t clusters_cone;  /* meshlet path, when it lands             */[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t draws;          /* non-empty buckets                       */[m
[32m+[m[32m} SceneCounters;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* GPU-written counters, read back with a fixed lag. */[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_counters_read(Scene *s, SceneCounters *out);   /* true if a fresh sample is ready */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mBackend piece needed: `bool buffer_readback(VkBackend*, VkCommandBuffer, BufferSlice dst_host,[m
[32m+[m[32mBufferSlice src_device, VkDeviceSize size)` plus a small ring of host-visible buffers keyed on the[m
[32m+[m[32msubmission timeline value, so a read is available 2 frames later with no stall. The existing[m
[32m+[m[32m`texture_readback` and the capture path already establish the pattern; the timeline semaphore already[m
[32m+[m[32mexists (`vk.h`: `timeline`, `timeline_last_submitted`).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mAlso wanted: named GPU timer scopes around `cull`, `hiz`, `draw`, and one per effect, so the profiler[m
[32m+[m[32mpanel can attribute time. `GpuProfiler` and `GPU_SCOPE` already exist; the new calls simply need to be[m
[32m+[m[32mplaced, and the panel needs a "show by default" flag so a screenshot is evidence.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 4. The refined public API[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mComplete signatures. Types not shown are unchanged from today.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 4.1 `vk.h` delta[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct BufferAccess { BufferSlice slice; VkPipelineStageFlags2 stage; VkAccessFlags2 access; } BufferAccess;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* PassDesc gains: push (bytes), buffer reads, buffer writes. Nothing else changes. */[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct PassDesc {[m
[32m+[m[32m    const PassAttachment *colors;         uint32_t color_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const PassAttachment *depth;[m
[32m+[m[32m    RenderTarget *const  *shader_reads;   uint32_t shader_read_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    RenderTarget *const  *shader_writes;  uint32_t shader_write_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const BufferAccess   *buf_reads;      uint32_t buf_read_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const BufferAccess   *buf_writes;     uint32_t buf_write_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    ByteSpan              push;           /* bytes, <= 256, pushed once at bind */[m
[32m+[m[32m    PipelineID            pipeline;[m
[32m+[m[32m} PassDesc;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Draw helpers lose their root data when the pass already pushed it. Passing an[m
[32m+[m[32m   empty ByteSpan means "keep the pass's constants". */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid cmd_draw_indexed_indirect(VkBackend *r, VkCommandBuffer cmd, ByteSpan root,[m
[32m+[m[32m                               BufferSlice indirect, uint32_t draw_count, uint32_t stride);[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid cmd_bind_index_buffer(VkCommandBuffer cmd, VkDeviceAddress indices, VkIndexType width);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Host-visible readback ring with timeline retirement. */[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct ReadbackSlot { Buffer host; uint64_t retire_value; bool pending; } ReadbackSlot;[m
[32m+[m[32mbool vk_readback_begin(VkBackend *r, VkCommandBuffer cmd, BufferSlice device_src, VkDeviceSize size,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       uint32_t *out_slot);[m
[32m+[m[32mbool vk_readback_consume(VkBackend *r, uint32_t slot, ByteSpan out);   /* false until retired */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 4.2 `scene.h` (replaces the `scene3d_*` surface)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* ---- lifetime ---- */[m
[32m+[m[32mScene *scene_create(VkBackend *vk, const SceneDesc *desc);   /* capacities, pool tag */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid   scene_destroy(Scene *scene);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* ---- assets (immutable, GPU resident) ---- */[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t   scene_mesh_set_add(Scene *s, const MeshSetDesc *desc);      /* device buffers + mesh table */[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t   scene_material_add(Scene *s, const MaterialDesc *desc);[m
[32m+[m[32mVkDeviceAddress scene_index_stream(const Scene *s);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* ---- instances (persistent, dirty-upload) ---- */[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_instance_reserve(Scene *s, uint32_t count, uint32_t *out_first);[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_upload_instances(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, Span(const InstanceUpdate) updates);[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_instance_free(Scene *s, uint32_t first, uint32_t count);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* ---- frame ---- */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_frame_begin(Scene *s, const SceneFrameDesc *frame);   /* capacity checks only */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_view_set(Scene *s, uint32_t view, const SceneViewDesc *view_desc);[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Cull is a pass: declares its own reads/writes, no hand-written barriers.[m
[32m+[m[32m   GPU owns visibility, LOD, compaction, command generation, and draw count. */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_cull(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, uint32_t view);[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Issues the view's single vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount over the compacted[m
[32m+[m[32m   commands. Caller owns the pass (it may add targets). */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_draw(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, uint32_t view);[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_counters(const Scene *s, SceneCounters *out);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* ---- policy switches, each one mappable to a before/after measurement ---- */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_set_occlusion(Scene *s, OcclusionMode mode);   /* OFF / HIZ_PREV_FRAME (default, §9.8); no two-phase pass */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_disable_occlusion(Scene *s, uint32_t frames);  /* camera-cut hatch, Q29: ignore Hi-Z for N frames */[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_set_lod_bias(Scene *s, float bias);[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_set_lod_enabled(Scene *s, bool on);[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mDesign notes on that surface:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `scene_cull(cmd, view)` is a *pass*, so the barrier between cull output and the[m
[32m+[m[32m  draw is the backend's job (3.6). The caller does not know it exists.[m
[32m+[m[32m- `scene_draw` issues the view's `Count` draw (§9.1) and does not open a pass:[m
[32m+[m[32m  the caller opened one (with any extra targets[m
[32m+[m[32m  for toon/object-id/normals), so MRT costs nothing in the API.[m
[32m+[m[32m- `Span(const InstanceUpdate)` rather than pointer + count, per doctrine.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Every performance switch is a named mode with an `OFF` value, so the A/B[m
[32m+[m[32m  measurement is one line in the game, not a recompile.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 4.3 What the game has to give up[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe game (`main.c`) keeps its `SceneInstanceSource` array as CPU truth. What changes:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- It no longer passes `Span(const SceneInstanceSource)` to the renderer every frame.[m
[32m+[m[32m  It calls `scene_upload_instances` for what moved and nothing otherwise.[m
[32m+[m[32m- It no longer receives `last_instances` / `last_candidates` / `last_draws` from the[m
[32m+[m[32m  stage; it asks `scene_counters` and gets GPU truth at a known lag.[m
[32m+[m[32m- It no longer owns `SceneCamera`'s clip rows: the camera produces a `SceneViewDesc`[m
[32m+[m[32m  (position, orientation, fov, near, far) and the stage builds rows plus anything[m
[32m+[m[32m  else the shaders want. Camera *integration* (yaw/pitch/speed/orbit) stays in the[m
[32m+[m[32m  game because that is gameplay.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 5. Before and after, in bytes (cubepets, 4096 instances, 6 meshes)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m| item | today | target | delta |[m
[32m+[m[32m|---|---:|---:|---|[m
[32m+[m[32m| instance record | 80 B | 20 B | -75% |[m
[32m+[m[32m| instance array per frame | 327,680 B uploaded + read | 81,920 B read, ~19,656 B uploaded (dirty 20%) | -94% upload |[m
[32m+[m[32m| candidate record | 8 B x 24,576 = 196,608 B | 0 B (Q6: dispatch ids derive the pair) | -100% |[m
[32m+[m[32m| draw record | 32 B | 32 B (§9.1/§9.9; initial `instanceCount = 1`, batchable later) | stride frozen, shape not |[m
[32m+[m[32m| material | 32 B | 12 B | -62% |[m
[32m+[m[32m| push per frame | 248 B x 7 = 1,736 B | 24 B x (1 cull + 1 draw) = 48 B | -97% |[m
[32m+[m[32m| CPU Vulkan calls in the scene pass | 6 x (bind + push + draw) + 2 barriers | 1 bind + 1 `Count` draw + 1 pass barrier per view (§9.1) | |[m
[32m+[m[32m| per-frame GPU sync points | 0 | 0 | (upstream fukuna: 1 blocking `vkQueueWaitIdle` every frame) |[m
[32m+[m[32m| vertex traffic | ~408 MB/frame | LOD2 for half the instances: ~250 MB, plus cone/Hi-Z savings | the actual win |[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 6. Rejected alternatives[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m1. **`vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount` / GPU-written draw count.** *Reversed by §9[m
[32m+[m[32m   (2026-09-27):* with GPU-side command compaction + per-pair LOD, the bucket count[m
[32m+[m[32m   is the surviving-pair count - which is visible only to the GPU. The `Count`[m
[32m+[m[32m   dependency (`vk.c:316`) is accepted and the backend prototype[m
[32m+[m[32m   (`cmd_draw_indexed_indirect_count`, `vk.c:2629`) is kept. The earlier reason[m
[32m+[m[32m   ("CPU knows mesh_count x LOD") no longer applies because static buckets are gone.[m
[32m+[m[32m2. **One instance per draw (`instanceCount = 1`), as in `threedgame`.** *Superseded[m
[32m+[m[32m   by §9.1/§9.9 (2026-09-27):* one command per surviving pair is the initial[m
[32m+[m[32m   command-generation shape, not a contract; it makes the[m
[32m+[m[32m   per-draw data record 128 B and destroys batching; the only thing it buys is that[m
[32m+[m[32m   the transform can live in the draw record. Batching survivors into one[m
[32m+[m[32m   command per (mesh, LOD) with `instanceCount > 1` stays open (§9.9).[m
[32m+[m[32m3. **CPU-built indirect command arrays, as in `fukuna_engine`.** 16 B per[m
[32m+[m[32m   (instance, mesh) written by the CPU, plus the CPU knowing visibility, plus a[m
[32m+[m[32m   blocking upload. This is the thing the design exists to delete.[m
[32m+[m[32m4. **Non-indexed draws with manual index fetches.** Doubles vertex traffic.[m
[32m+[m[32m5. **Padding every push struct to 256 bytes** (their `PUSH_CONSTANT` macro).[m
[32m+[m[32m6. **Storing per-instance world bounds.** Derive them from the mesh's local bounds[m
[32m+[m[32m   and the instance basis: 16 B/instance saved, at the cost of 2 ALU ops per[m
[32m+[m[32m   candidate in a kernel that is memory-bound.[m
[32m+[m[32m7. **Tracking buffer state per region.** Whole-buffer state is enough at this pool[m
[32m+[m[32m   granularity; if it ever isn't, the change is additive.[m
[32m+[m[32m8. **A separate "render graph" layer.** The pass API plus an explicit view array is[m
[32m+[m[32m   enough. Upstream's `high-level-rendering-using-render-graphs` doc proposes more[m
[32m+[m[32m   machinery than the current frame shape needs, and doctrine says no abstraction[m
[32m+[m[32m   without a concrete need.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 7. Questions I need answered before this is buildable[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mGrouped. My recommendation follows each; the point of listing them is that several[m
[32m+[m[32mof these change the *layout*, and layouts are expensive to change later.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Layout and encoding**[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q1.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: chunk-relative f16 position, 20-B record[m
[32m+[m[32m  kept.** `pos[3]` is relative to a per-instance chunk origin; the[m
[32m+[m[32m  chunk id travels in the record's spare word and the chunk table lives[m
[32m+[m[32m  GPU-side. Raw world-space f16 (ulp 1.0 near 1024) was the thing that[m
[32m+[m[32m  made large worlds fatal; chunk-relative keeps both precision and the[m
[32m+[m[32m  compact layout.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q2.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: quantized TRS, kept.** 4 B/instance cheaper[m
[32m+[m[32m  than 3 f16 affine rows, and shear is not a per-instance concept — the[m
[32m+[m[32m  importer bakes it into static mesh vertices at load (Q3).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q3.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: uniform per-instance scale.** `scale[3]`[m
[32m+[m[32m  becomes one half plus 4 spare bytes (`spare`, reserved for chunk id and[m
[32m+[m[32m  the like). The rule is: the renderer only ever sees translation +[m
[32m+[m[32m  rotation + uniform scale. The importer canonicalizes source node[m
[32m+[m[32m  transforms (TRS or matrix) before they reach `GpuInstance`: non-uniform[m
[32m+[m[32m  scale and shear are baked into static mesh vertices at load, negative[m
[32m+[m[32m  scale is resolved in the loader (it flips winding/culling), and skinned[m
[32m+[m[32m  meshes keep their skeleton/skin space coherent with the instance[m
[32m+[m[32m  transform held separate. The loader never refuses a mesh for[m
[32m+[m[32m  non-uniform scale; it bakes it. A precomputed per-instance world radius[m
[32m+[m[32m  into the spare bytes is rejected: an instance fans out to 1..4 meshes[m
[32m+[m[32m  with different local radii, so the world radius is per (instance, mesh),[m
[32m+[m[32m  not per instance — it stays derived as `local_radius * scale` in the[m
[32m+[m[32m  cull thread.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q4.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: keep AoS 20 B.** Cull reads every field per[m
[32m+[m[32m  instance and the VS reads the same record for visible ones — one array,[m
[32m+[m[32m  two readers, no duplicated bounds. SoA only wins if something reads[m
[32m+[m[32m  positions alone in bulk (streaming, shadow bounds), and we have no such[m
[32m+[m[32m  pass. Revisit only if such a pass appears.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q5.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: mirrored-plane frustum for normal[m
[32m+[m[32m  perspective cameras, generic 6-plane path as fallback.** Main camera[m
[32m+[m[32m  gets the fast path (§9.2); shadow / off-center / orthographic views[m
[32m+[m[32m  keep the 6-plane test. The VS never needs either.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q6.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: prefer derived dispatch ids while mesh-set[m
[32m+[m[32m  size stays bounded.** With `MAX_MESHES_PER_SET` of 4 or 8:[m
[32m+[m[32m  `iid = tid / MAX_MESHES_PER_SET`, `local_m = tid % MAX_MESHES_PER_SET`.[m
[32m+[m[32m  This removes the CPU candidate upload while keeping candidate-level[m
[32m+[m[32m  parallelism. Do not use it if mesh-set sizes become large or highly[m
[32m+[m[32m  variable. Note the scale of the prize: the current candidate upload is[m
[32m+[m[32m  only ~197 KB/frame, so the motivation is CPU work and GPU parallelism,[m
[32m+[m[32m  not bandwidth. Orthogonal to §9's `Count` move: the compacted command[m
[32m+[m[32m  needs the same (instance, mesh) enumeration either way.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q7.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: keep `visible[]` private.** Pack[m
[32m+[m[32m  `instance:16 | mesh:16` into u32 as the initial shape (0 extra bytes,[m
[32m+[m[32m  caps a view at 65535 visible instances); a secondary indirection only[m
[32m+[m[32m  if it ever bites. The 16/16 packing stays the initial shape only; the[m
[32m+[m[32m  entry format is implementation-private (Q28/§9.9).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q8.** Draw record stride 20 (37% fewer bytes, unaligned records) or 32[m
[32m+[m[32m  (aligned, matches Niagara's `MeshDrawCommand`, 12 B become a designated extension[m
[32m+[m[32m  field)? **Resolved by §9 (2026-09-27): 32**, asserted by `_Static_assert`.[m
[32m+[m[32m  Static buckets are gone, so the waste scales with survivors, not buckets.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q9.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: require `drawIndirectFirstInstance`.**[m
[32m+[m[32m  The visible-indexing scheme uses `firstInstance` as a base into[m
[32m+[m[32m  `visible[]`, so the Vulkan 1.1 feature stays a hard dependency.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q10.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: put LOD/cluster fields in the asset layout[m
[32m+[m[32m  now, allow empty.** `lodCount = 1` and `clusterCount = 0` until the mesh[m
[32m+[m[32m  pipeline can emit ladders/clusters; the shader needs no `#ifdef` and[m
[32m+[m[32m  LOD0 is always correct (§9.5).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q11.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: do not grow the 20-B instance record for[m
[32m+[m[32m  TAA.** If motion vectors are ever wanted, they come from a separate[m
[32m+[m[32m  sidecar (previous-transform ring or a velocity output), not from[m
[32m+[m[32m  widening `GpuInstance`. Q2's quantized-TRS answer stands unchanged.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q12.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: no 2 extra bytes — reuse the spare byte[m
[32m+[m[32m  as an 8-bit mesh-set id.** 256 sets instead of 16, record size[m
[32m+[m[32m  unchanged. The flags byte keeps bit0 dynamic, bit1 skinned, bit2[m
[32m+[m[32m  shadow, bit3 two-sided; the old bits4-7 mesh-set nibble moves to the[m
[32m+[m[32m  `reserved` byte, which becomes `mesh_set` (see §3.2).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Capacity and policy**[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q13.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: skinning stays an optional capability —[m
[32m+[m[32m  allocate only when used.** Skinning will be used (barbarian-style[m
[32m+[m[32m  rigid/bone-part characters count), so the design keeps the path, but[m
[32m+[m[32m  skinning buffers are allocated only when a scene actually contains[m
[32m+[m[32m  skinned meshes. Rigid bone-part characters do not need full vertex[m
[32m+[m[32m  skinning; the pose-dedupe rule (§3.8) still applies when the path is[m
[32m+[m[32m  wired.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q14.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: runtime capacity at `scene_create()`,[m
[32m+[m[32m  hard maximum, allocate once.** Never resize during gameplay; capacity[m
[32m+[m[32m  is a creation argument capped at `SCENE3D_MAX_INSTANCES` (65536,[m
[32m+[m[32m  1.3 MB at 20 B).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q15.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: runtime `view_count` with a fixed[m
[32m+[m[32m  `MAX_VIEWS` hard cap.** Do not bake the renderer around 1/4/6 views;[m
[32m+[m[32m  `GpuView[]` is sized by the cap, filled per frame up to `view_count`.[m
[32m+[m[32m  Each view owns its draws/visible/Hi-Z state (§3.7).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q16.** **Resolved (2026-09-27): keep previous-frame Hi-Z.** Same-frame[m
[32m+[m[32m  two-phase (draw occluders, build Hi-Z, cull the rest) is rejected: it costs a[m
[32m+[m[32m  mid-frame barrier and a two-tier instance classification for removing an artifact[m
[32m+[m[32m  class that the 1-frame lag bounds to camera cuts. The accepted artifact is one[m
[32m+[m[32m  frame of pop-in on newly-visible geometry after a cut; a[m
[32m+[m[32m  `scene_disable_occlusion(frames)` hatch (Q29) covers teleports.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q17.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: Hi-Z is a renderer service, per-system[m
[32m+[m[32m  buffers stay separate.** The pyramid builder is shared/generic (2D,[m
[32m+[m[32m  particles, post can all consume it), but each system owns its buffers;[m
[32m+[m[32m  no shared mutable Hi-Z state.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q18.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: keep GPU counters, debug/profiling only,[m
[32m+[m[32m  never a sync point.** The delayed-readback ring (2-frame lag, §3.9)[m
[32m+[m[32m  already heads the right way: counters retire on the timeline and must[m
[32m+[m[32m  never stall the render path.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**API shape and code organisation**[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q19.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: split into `scene_assets.c`,[m
[32m+[m[32m  `scene_instances.c`, `scene_pass.c`** (replacing `src/three_d/scene3d_*`)[m
[32m+[m[32m  plus `shaders/cull.slang`, `shaders/scene.slang`, `shaders/hiz.slang`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q20.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: keep 2D and 3D separate implementations;[m
[32m+[m[32m  share conventions, not buffers.** Same barrier model and visible-array[m
[32m+[m[32m  convention, separate code — keeps 2D out of a 3D app's memory, which is[m
[32m+[m[32m  the property `GameHooks.two_d` exists to guarantee.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q21.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: game owns explicit dirty lists.** Keep[m
[32m+[m[32m  `SceneInstanceSource` (52 B, CPU) as the game's truth, but never diff[m
[32m+[m[32m  all instances per frame — the game knows what moved and uploads only[m
[32m+[m[32m  that (§3.8).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q22.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: static maximum draw capacity, GPU-written[m
[32m+[m[32m  actual count.** The "no `Count` variant" rule was dropped by §9:[m
[32m+[m[32m  `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount` is the draw call with a static[m
[32m+[m[32m  `MAX_CANDIDATES` cap plus dropped-counter (Q26).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q23.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: GPU tables are authoritative.** Device[m
[32m+[m[32m  buffers written by the loader; the CPU may keep lightweight asset[m
[32m+[m[32m  metadata for editor/debug tooling but never a second mutable rendering[m
[32m+[m[32m  state.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q24.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: `scene_create` can fail (invalid handle),[m
[32m+[m[32m  uploads return `bool`, internal invariant violations assert.**[m
[32m+[m[32m  `scene_upload_instances` is the one function that can legitimately fail[m
[32m+[m[32m  on external data.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q25.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: absolutely no `wait_idle` in the frame[m
[32m+[m[32m  loop.** Frame-lifetime resources are sized once at `scene_create` and[m
[32m+[m[32m  recreated only through an explicit resource-lifetime path outside the[m
[32m+[m[32m  frame (resize/reallocation).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 8. Summary[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- Both upstream snapshots are CPU-driven for the scene: fukuna builds indirect[m
[32m+[m[32m  commands on the CPU and stalls the queue every frame to upload them; threedgame[m
[32m+[m[32m  issues one draw per mesh with `instanceCount = 1` and 128 bytes of per-mesh draw[m
[32m+[m[32m  data re-uploaded every frame, with a CPU-written draw count. Neither has a cull[m
[32m+[m[32m  shader. Our port is already past both on the axis that defines a GPU-driven[m
[32m+[m[32m  renderer.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The refined design keeps the two things our port got right (GPU-owned[m
[32m+[m[32m  visibility, one indirect draw per view under 9 (per bucket in the pre-Count 3 text), the pass API and image tracker) and fixes[m
[32m+[m[32m  the three it did not finish: the 248-byte push that mixes frame and per-draw data,[m
[32m+[m[32m  the 80-byte instance record with stored bounds, and per-draw Vulkan calls.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Target record sizes: instance 20 B, mesh 40 B, material 12 B, draw 32 B stride[m
[32m+[m[32m  (§9.1/§9.9; initial `instanceCount = 1`, batchable later), push 24 B[m
[32m+[m[32m  once per pass. Visible entries pack `instance:16 | mesh:16` initially (§9.9;[m
[32m+[m[32m  format stays implementation-private, Q28) so the VS needs no[m
[32m+[m[32m  per-draw constant.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The real performance work is LOD plus occlusion (previous-frame Hi-Z, 9.8) plus clusters, because vertex and[m
[32m+[m[32m  index traffic is ~1000x the metadata traffic. Everything else here is CPU time and[m
[32m+[m[32m  cleanliness.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **All of Q1-Q29 are answered (2026-09-27, `gpuans.txt`)**; the layout[m
[32m+[m[32m  decisions (Q1 chunk-relative f16, Q2 quantized TRS, Q3 uniform scale,[m
[32m+[m[32m  Q4 AoS, Q12 8-bit mesh-set) and the frame-contract decisions[m
[32m+[m[32m  (Q16 previous-frame Hi-Z, Q22 static cap + `Count`, Q26 clamp +[m
[32m+[m[32m  dropped-counter, Q27/Q28 private compaction and `visible[]`) are locked[m
[32m+[m[32m  in §7. The design is implementable without further decisions.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m---[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m## 9. GPU-driven `Count` (Niagara-inspired) - supersedes 3.4/3.5, §6 item 1, Q6/Q8[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m**Decision:** move to `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount`. The GPU owns visibility,[m
[32m+[m[32mLOD, compaction, command generation (including the `instanceCount` /[m
[32m+[m[32m`firstInstance` fields), and the draw count; the CPU issues **one** draw[m
[32m+[m[32mcommand per view; LOD + occlusion + compaction are first-class - no task/mesh[m
[32m+[m[32mshaders, no two-pass occlusion, no hardware mesh shading.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mTaken from `niagara/` (zeux's streamed Vulkan renderer, vendored in-tree):[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m| Niagara mechanism | File | Adopt? |[m
[32m+[m[32m|---|---|---|[m
[32m+[m[32m| `MeshDraw` per-draw record (TRS + mesh/material refs) | `src/shaders/mesh.h:92` | shape only; ours is the 20 B quantized TRS |[m
[32m+[m[32m| `MeshDrawCommand { drawId, VkDrawIndexedIndirectCommand }` at stride 32, written by `atomicAdd(commandCount,1)` | `mesh.h:104`, `drawcull.comp.glsl:143` | **yes, exactly** - GPU-compacted dense commands |[m
[32m+[m[32m| `MeshTaskCommand` + indirect task dispatch | `mesh.h:116`, `tasksubmit.comp.glsl` | **no** - exists only to feed mesh shaders we will not compile |[m
[32m+[m[32m| Two-pass (early/late) occlusion | `niagara.cpp:1771-1784` | **no** - we cull once against previous-frame Hi-Z |[m
[32m+[m[32m| `drawVisibility[di]` ping-pong array | `drawcull.comp.glsl:154-155` | **no** - two-pass bookkeeping only |[m
[32m+[m[32m| `projectSphere` + `getOcclusionMip` + mirrored-plane frustum | `shaders/math.h:2-39` | **yes**, the mip logic and the frustum form |[m
[32m+[m[32m| Center-of-AABB single sample at chosen mip, linear fetch with min-reduction sampler | `drawcull.comp.glsl:91-94` | **no** - our 2x2 `Load` is strictly more conservative and sampler-free |[m
[32m+[m[32m| Per-draw LOD error ladder (`lods[8].error`) | `mesh.h:53-60`, `drawcull:106-116` | **yes**, the metric, with our own ladder (9.5) |[m
[32m+[m[32m| Cluster cone culling, int8 cone axis + cutoff | `mesh.h:11-24`, `clustercull:75-104` | **later** - needs clusterized assets + meshlet table |[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.1 New frame contract[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mOne dispatch per view. The cull kernel **compacts** instead of filling buckets.[m
[32m+[m[32mGPU owns visibility, LOD, compaction, command generation, and draw count; the[m
[32m+[m[32mCPU issues one `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount` per view. Initial implementation[m
[32m+[m[32mshape (not a contract):[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32m/* per-view: one command counter + one visible-slot counter, shared by the view */[m
[32m+[m[32muint dci = atomicAdd(commandCount, 1);                 /* cf. Niagara drawcull:143, initial strategy only, see Q27 */[m
[32m+[m[32mdrawCommands[dci].drawId        = candidate.draw_id;   /* mesh (+LOD) identity */[m
[32m+[m[32mdrawCommands[dci].indexCount    = lod.indexCount;      /* LOD-selected */[m
[32m+[m[32mdrawCommands[dci].instanceCount = 1;                   /* initial shape only, see 9.9 */[m
[32m+[m[32mdrawCommands[dci].firstIndex    = lod.indexOffset;[m
[32m+[m[32mdrawCommands[dci].vertexOffset  = mesh.vertexOffset;[m
[32m+[m[32mdrawCommands[dci].firstInstance = base;                /* base into visible[], see 9.9 */[m
[32m+[m[32mvisible[base]                   = iid | (mesh_slot << 16); /* initial packing only, see 9.9 */[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe initial build emits one command per surviving (instance, mesh) pair.[m
[32m+[m[32m**Anchor decision (2026-09-27):** `MeshDrawCommand` is 32 B (20 B[m
[32m+[m[32m`VkDrawIndexedIndirectCommand` + 12 B designated extension field) and the[m
[32m+[m[32mdraw's push root is **empty** - identity comes only from `visible[]` plus the[m
[32m+[m[32mview constants. Draw count is `commandCount`, GPU-written, consumed by[m
[32m+[m[32m`vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount(cmd, commands, 0, count, 0, MAX_CMDS, 32)`.[m
[32m+[m[32mBackend support already exists verbatim: `cmd_draw_indexed_indirect_count`[m
[32m+[m[32m(`vk.c:2629`); the `multi_draw_indirect_count` feature is `TRY_ENABLE`d[m
[32m+[m[32m(`vk.c:316`).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mWhat is frozen here is the frame contract (GPU compacts, GPU counts, one[m
[32m+[m[32m`Count` draw per view) - not the command shape. Command generation is designed[m
[32m+[m[32mso a later pass can batch surviving instances into one command per (mesh, LOD)[m
[32m+[m[32mwith `instanceCount > 1` and a `firstInstance` base into a contiguous[m
[32m+[m[32m`visible[]` range, with no public-API or VS-identity change. Likewise the[m
[32m+[m[32m`visible[]` entry format and the per-survivor `atomicAdd` above are initial[m
[32m+[m[32mchoices only; both stay implementation-private (Q27/Q28, §9.9).[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m`max_draw_count` is a static cap (`MAX_CANDIDATES`), so the CPU needs no[m
[32m+[m[32mvisibility knowledge. All per-mesh draws collapse into **one draw call per view**.[m
[32m+[m[32mCost model of the initial strategy: one global `atomicAdd` per surviving pair,[m
[32m+[m[32msame as Niagara's `drawcull.comp.glsl:143` - contention is bounded by survivors,[m
[32m+[m[32mnot submissions.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.2 Steal the mirrored-plane frustum test[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mNiagara tests two frustum sides at once from camera space, using only the four[m
[32m+[m[32msymmetric planes plus explicit near/far (`drawcull.comp.glsl:79-82`):[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mvisible = visible && center.z * frustum[1] - abs(center.x) * frustum[0] > -radius;[m
[32m+[m[32mvisible = visible && center.z * frustum[3] - abs(center.y) * frustum[2] > -radius;[m
[32m+[m[32mvisible = visible && center.z + radius > znear && center.z - radius < zfar;[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m`frustum[]` is filled once per frame from the normalized projection row sums[m
[32m+[m[32m(`niagara.cpp:1502-1514`: `(row3+row0)`, `(row3+row1)`). This is tighter than our[m
[32m+[m[32msix-plane `Frustum`, needs 4 dot-equivalents instead of 6, no per-plane[m
[32m+[m[32mnormalization, and no 96 bytes of push data. The per-view push shrinks by exactly[m
[32m+[m[32mthe frustum block. Adopt verbatim; keep our `frustum_extract` only for the C-side[m
[32m+[m[32munit test.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.3 Keep the 2x2 `Load`, but with their comment[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mNiagara samples the pyramid **once**, at the AABB center, with a linear-filtered[m
[32m+[m[32m`textureLod` on a sampler whose reduction mode is min (`drawcull:93-94`):[m
[32m+[m[32m*"Sampler is set up to do min reduction, so this computes the minimum depth of a[m
[32m+[m[32m2x2 texel quad."* That needs a custom border-aware sampler with[m
[32m+[m[32m`VK_SAMPLER_REDUCTION_MODE_MIN`, and linear filtering on depth textures is[m
[32m+[m[32mimplementation-defined on some drivers - Niagara accepts both, and its[m
[32m+[m[32m`depthSampler` has to be wired per pyramid binding.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mOur `cs_main` fetches the 2x2 explicitly with `Load` and takes the min, then[m
[32m+[m[32mrequires `m >= depth_max`. Strictly more conservative, sampler-free, no[m
[32m+[m[32mreduction-mode sampler. **Keep it.** The one steal from Niagara is its mip-selection[m
[32m+[m[32mcorrection: after `level = ceil(log2(span))`, test whether the rect straddles at[m
[32m+[m[32mmost 2 texels at `level-1` and drop down (`math.h:35-36`) - a one-level-finer fetch[m
[32m+[m[32mis free and cuts false negatives roughly 2x. Our current `hi - lo <= 1 … else[m
[32m+[m[32mskip` covers the correctness half; the correction covers the precision half.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.4 What this deletes or supersedes from Section 3[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mSuperseded by §9, not deleted silently — the following §3 text now reads as[m
[32m+[m[32mpre-`Count` history:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- 3.3's "one `instanceCount` per mesh bucket" write target. Buckets are gone;[m
[32m+[m[32m  commands are compacted.[m
[32m+[m[32m- 3.4's "one draw per mesh slot" vocabulary and its `visible[i] =[m
[32m+[m[32m  instance_slot | (mesh_slot << 16)` packing, its per-draw VS mesh-fetch[m
[32m+[m[32m  walkthrough, and its `cmd_draw_indexed_indirect(cmd, draws_slice,[m
[32m+[m[32m  view->draw_count, 20, ...)` draw loop (3.4, lines ~397-439). The `Count`[m
[32m+[m[32m  frame contract is §9.1: one `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount` per view over[m
[32m+[m[32m  GPU-compacted commands; the initial `visible[]` packing is §9.1/§9.9, and[m
[32m+[m[32m  the format itself stays implementation-private (Q28).[m
[32m+[m[32m- 3.4's per-draw LOD-bucket layout (`mesh_slot * LOD_COUNT + lod`, 3.5) is[m
[32m+[m[32m  replaced by per-pair LOD selection written into the compacted command[m
[32m+[m[32m  (§9.1, §9.5); the (mesh, LOD) batching of `instanceCount > 1` survivors is[m
[32m+[m[32m  an allowed future inside command generation (§9.9), not a bucket revival.[m
[32m+[m[32m- 3.5's "CPU still knows the bucket count statically, which keeps[m
[32m+[m[32m  `draw_indexed_indirect` viable and keeps the «no Count variant» rule[m
[32m+[m[32m  intact" no longer holds: static buckets are gone and the `Count` variant[m
[32m+[m[32m  is the draw call (§9.1).[m
[32m+[m[32m- 6.1, in full. Count *is* GPU-written; the `Count` feature dependency is accepted[m
[32m+[m[32m  and already requested in `vk.c:316`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- Q8 (stride): **anchored 2026-09-27 at 32**, matching `MeshDrawCommand` plus a designated[m
[32m+[m[32m  extension field, asserted by `_Static_assert`.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The `first_instance`-is-visible-base convention stays: each compacted command[m
[32m+[m[32m  carries a `firstInstance` base into `visible[]` plus its `instanceCount`.[m
[32m+[m[32m  Initially one command per surviving pair (`instanceCount = 1`, so `base`[m
[32m+[m[32m  is the command's own output index and no slot allocator is needed); a future[m
[32m+[m[32m  pass may batch survivors per (mesh, LOD) with `instanceCount > 1` over a[m
[32m+[m[32m  contiguous `visible[]` range (§9.9) — at that point the visible-slot allocator[m
[32m+[m[32m  comes back, without touching the public API or the VS identity path.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.5 LOD policy (with Q10's data, without Q10's dependency)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mNiagara's metric: `threshold = distance * lodTarget / scale`, walk the ladder[m
[32m+[m[32mwhile `mesh.lods[i].error < threshold` (`drawcull:110-115`). Ours:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m```c[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat  dist   = length(center) - radius;            /* view space, > 0 */[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat  metric = dist * lodTarget / inst.scale;   /* uniform scale, Q3 */[m
[32m+[m[32muint   lod    = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32mwhile (lod + 1 < mesh.lodCount && mesh.lods[lod + 1].error < metric) ++lod;[m
[32m+[m[32m```[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- `lodTarget` is a per-view float (same units Niagara uses: "lod target error at[m
[32m+[m[32m  z=1"), exposed via `scene_set_lod_target` - name the units in the header.[m
[32m+[m[32m- `mesh.lods[]` is what the loader fills; until then `lodCount = 1` and the walk[m
[32m+[m[32m  is a no-op - the shader needs no `#ifdef`, and LOD0 is always correct.[m
[32m+[m[32m- The command's `indexCount`/`firstIndex` for the chosen LOD are the only write[m
[32m+[m[32m  targets; there is no `(mesh x LOD)` static slot explosion.[m
[32m+[m[32m- A pixel-size floor (`projected_diameter < 2px` => deepest LOD or cull) is a[m
[32m+[m[32m  one-line addition later; Niagara does not do it, and we flag it as a future[m
[32m+[m[32m  measurement before adding a branch.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.6 New questions this raises[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q26.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: clamp at capacity, count[m
[32m+[m[32m  `dropped_commands`, never silently overflow.** `commandCount` clamps at[m
[32m+[m[32m  `MAX_CANDIDATES` (Niagara: *"drop draw calls on overflow; this limits us[m
[32m+[m[32m  to ~4M"*, `drawcull:128-129`); the dropped count stays visible so[m
[32m+[m[32m  overflows are never silent.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q27.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: start with global-atomic compaction;[m
[32m+[m[32m  keep the shader structured so subgroup/workgroup prefix can replace it[m
[32m+[m[32m  without an API change.** Per-survivor `atomicAdd(commandCount)` first[m
[32m+[m[32m  for simplicity (Niagara-fast); the prefix form is an[m
[32m+[m[32m  implementation-private optimization triggered by profiling. Do not[m
[32m+[m[32m  hard-code subgroup-size assumptions (no "group of 64" baked into the[m
