[32m+[m[32m  shader); size the prefix math off the actual workgroup/subgroup size.[m
[32m+[m[32m  The public contract is only: survivors become a dense command array,[m
[32m+[m[32m  `commandCount` is GPU-written. The VS never sees the strategy[m
[32m+[m[32m  (§9.7 item 1 is the pre-approved form of that switch).[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q28.** **Resolved 2026-09-27: `visible[]` representation stays[m
[32m+[m[32m  implementation-private.** The public contract is only: each command's[m
[32m+[m[32m  `firstInstance` is a base into `visible[]`, and the VS resolves[m
[32m+[m[32m  (instance, mesh/LOD) from the entry. Neither `instanceCount = 1` nor the[m
[32m+[m[32m  16/16 packing is frozen: command generation may later batch survivors into one[m
[32m+[m[32m  command per (mesh, LOD) with `instanceCount > 1` and a `visible[]` base, with[m
[32m+[m[32m  no VS or public-API change.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **Q29.** **Resolved 2026-09-27, renamed: `scene_disable_occlusion(frames)`.**[m
[32m+[m[32m  With previous-frame Hi-Z locked in (Q16), newly-visible geometry after a[m
[32m+[m[32m  camera cut pops in one frame late. A camera cut is an occlusion-history[m
[32m+[m[32m  problem, not an LOD problem — hence the rename from[m
[32m+[m[32m  `scene_force_full_detail`: it disables occlusion for N frames after a[m
[32m+[m[32m  teleport. One function, zero cost when unused — see the locked[m
[32m+[m[32m  invariants in §9.8. Include it in the `scene.h` surface.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.7 Suggested further optimizations (beyond Niagara, still no mesh shaders)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m1. **Subgroup ballot compaction instead of `InterlockedAdd` per pair.** Inside a[m
[32m+[m[32m   workgroup, `ballot(visible)` + a subgroup prefix gives each surviving thread[m
[32m+[m[32m   its group-local offset with one `atomicAdd(commandCount, group_total)` per[m
[32m+[m[32m   group. Fewer atomics in dense views for ~6 instructions of prefix math; size[m
[32m+[m[32m   the prefix off the actual workgroup/subgroup size, never a hard-coded 64[m
[32m+[m[32m   (Q27). Do this only if Q27's measurement says the global atomic is hot.[m
[32m+[m[32m2. **64-bit packed candidate keys.** Pack `(depth_metric:32 | draw_id:32)` per[m
[32m+[m[32m   survivor and radix-sort before writing commands: front-to-back order cuts[m
[32m+[m[32m   overdraw ~2x in dense crowds and improves Hi-Z hit rate on *this* frame's pass.[m
[32m+[m[32m   Cost: one counting-sort pass over a dense array (already proven in the 2D[m
[32m+[m[32m   sprite path's batch sort). Biggest pure-win after LOD; requires a scratch[m
[32m+[m[32m   buffer of `MAX_CANDIDATES` u64.[m
[32m+[m[32m3. **Cluster-level backface cone test in the cull thread** (Chevy's cone math is[m
[32m+[m[32m   in `niagara/src/shaders/math.h:41`, the int8 cone data in `mesh.h:15-16`).[m
[32m+[m[32m   Our per-mesh cull can reject a whole draw for back-facing single-cluster[m
[32m+[m[32m   meshes (small props, heads) for free once Q10 feeds the cone table - no[m
[32m+[m[32m   meshlet dispatch needed, one dot product per candidate.[m
[32m+[m[32m4. **Depth pre-pass toggle for the nearest 5%.** A `COUNT`-driven pre-pass that[m
[32m+[m[32m   draws only the front-most surviving pairs (sorted by `depth_metric` from (2))[m
[32m+[m[32m   builds a same-frame occluder set without Niagara's two-pass machinery: occluders[m
[32m+[m[32m   render with color writes off, then the main `Count` draw consumes fresh depth.[m
[32m+[m[32m   Strictly better than previous-frame Hi-Z for static geometry; keep[m
[32m+[m[32m   previous-frame as the default and the pre-pass as a per-view flag[m
[32m+[m[32m   (`OcclusionMode`, 4.2) because it doubles vertex cost for the occluders.[m
[32m+[m[32m5. **Persistent mapped instance buffer + GPU-side dirty merge.** Instead of the CPU[m
[32m+[m[32m   uploading `InstanceUpdate[]` (3.8), keep instances in one persistently-mapped[m
[32m+[m[32m   buffer and let the cull kernel read a small GPU-side "generation counter" array:[m
[32m+[m[32m   instances whose generation != frame generation are skipped without CPU upload.[m
[32m+[m[32m   Removes the upload path entirely for static scenes; costs one u32 read per[m
[32m+[m[32m   instance per frame. Only matters if update traffic, not read traffic, dominates[m
[32m+[m[32m   - measure via `scene_counters`.[m
[32m+[m[32m6. **Shared `CullData` push across cull + Hi-Z build + debug passes.** Niagara[m
[32m+[m[32m   pushes one `Globals`/`CullData` block (`mesh.h:26-51`) to every compute entry;[m
[32m+[m[32m   our three entries (`cs_main`, `cs_hiz`, cull-debug) should share an identical[m
[32m+[m[32m   128 B prefix so one push call serves the whole chain. Concretely: `rows[4]` is[m
[32m+[m[32m   wrong as push content - push the *view matrix* (64 B) + 4 scalars (frustum,[m
[32m+[m[32m   P00/P11, znear, lodTarget) + pyramid size, and let shaders derive symmetric[m
[32m+[m[32m   planes; that is 96 B total and drops the 96 B `Frustum` from every push.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.8 Hi-Z invariants (locked 2026-09-27)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mTwo rules, both free, that keep previous-frame Hi-Z (Q16) correct without a[m
[32m+[m[32msame-frame accumulation pass:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m1. **Transparent passes run with depth-write off whenever Hi-Z is on.**[m
[32m+[m[32m   Non-monotonic depth (a later pass writing depth our pyramid read earlier) only[m
[32m+[m[32m   exists if a transparent pass writes depth *after* the Hi-Z build. The rule[m
[32m+[m[32m   removes the case entirely: zero GPU cost, zero branches, enforced as a[m
[32m+[m[32m   render-state invariant. Any effect that genuinely needs depth-writing[m
[32m+[m[32m   transparents opts out of Hi-Z per pass via the `OcclusionMode` switch (§4.2)[m
[32m+[m[32m   instead of paying for a handling path in the cull.[m
[32m+[m[32m2. **`scene_disable_occlusion(frames)` covers camera cuts (Q29).** Newly-visible[m
[32m+[m[32m   geometry after a teleport pops in at most one frame late under previous-frame[m
[32m+[m[32m   Hi-Z; the hatch disables culling for N frames after a teleport. Zero cost when[m
[32m+[m[32m   unused, one function in the `scene.h` surface.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mA same-frame scratch-pyramid update was considered and rejected: it cannot feed[m
[32m+[m[32mthe cull that already ran, so it needs a second full cull (or an occluder[m
[32m+[m[32mpre-pass — the two-phase Q16 rejected), roughly 2.5–3x the cull stage every[m
[32m+[m[32mframe, to fix a case that occurs on ~0% of normal frames.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m### 9.9 Command generation stays behind the API (locked 2026-09-27)[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe public contract is: GPU owns visibility, LOD, compaction, command[m
[32m+[m[32mgeneration, and draw count; `scene_cull()` computes, `vkCmdDrawIndexedIndirectCount`[m
[32m+[m[32mconsumes. Everything below it is an implementation choice, and the following are[m
[32m+[m[32mexplicitly **not** frozen:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m- **`instanceCount = 1` per command is today's shape, not a contract.** Command[m
[32m+[m[32m  generation may later batch surviving instances into one command per[m
[32m+[m[32m  (mesh, LOD) with `instanceCount > 1` and a `firstInstance` base into[m
[32m+[m[32m  `visible[]`. The VS already resolves identity through `visible[]`, so batching[m
[32m+[m[32m  changes atomics and slot assignment only.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **`visible[]` entry format is private.** Today `instance:16 | mesh:16`; tomorrow[m
[32m+[m[32m  a `(mesh, LOD)` key or a resolved address — the VS path reads whatever the[m
[32m+[m[32m  cull kernel writes, through the same `firstInstance` base. No public type[m
[32m+[m[32m  names the entry.[m
[32m+[m[32m- **The compaction strategy is private.** Per-survivor `atomicAdd(commandCount)`[m
[32m+[m[32m  today; subgroup/workgroup prefix (Q27) later without touching the VS, the[m
[32m+[m[32m  public headers, or the frame contract.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThe one invariant that must hold across all three: **the draw-count buffer, the[m
[32m+[m[32mcommand array, and `visible[]` are a consistent triple after `scene_cull()`[m
[32m+[m[32mreturns** — `count` commands, each pointing at its own `visible[]` range.[m
[32m+[m[32mSub-question Q26 (overflow policy) therefore stays public: clamping at[m
[32m+[m[32m`MAX_CANDIDATES` with a dropped counter, until a scene proves otherwise.[m

[32m+[m[32mQ2: Quantized TRS. Keep it.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ3: Uniform per-instance scale. Keep it.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ4: AoS. Keep it.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ5: Use mirrored-plane frustum for normal perspective cameras, with the generic 6-plane path as a fallback for shadow/off-center/orthographic views. Main camera gets the fast path.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mQ6: Prefer derived dispatch IDs when mesh-set size is bounded, e.g. 4 or 8 meshes:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32miid     = tid / MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m[32mlocal_m = tid % MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mThis removes the CPU candidate upload while keeping candidate-level parallelism. Don't use it if mesh-set sizes become large or highly variable. Your current candidate upload is only ~197 KB/frame, so the motivation is mostly CPU work and keeping the GPU parallel, not bandwidth.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mQ7: Keep visible[] private.[m

[32m+[m[32mQ9: Require drawIndirectFirstInstance.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ10: Put LOD/cluster fields in the asset layout now, but allow lodCount=1 and clusterCount=0.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ11: Don't grow GpuInstance for TAA/motion vectors. Use a sidecar/history buffer.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mQ12: Don't waste 2 extra bytes. Use the existing spare byte as an 8-bit mesh-set ID, giving you 256 sets instead of 16 without increasing the record size.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ13: Keep skinning as an optional capability because we will use skinning for sure, but allocate skinning buffers only when a scene actually uses skinned meshes. A barbarian-style rigid/bone-part character does not need full vertex skinning.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ14: Runtime capacity passed to scene_create(), with a hard maximum. Allocate once, never resize during gameplay.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ15: Runtime view_count with a fixed MAX_VIEWS hard cap. Don't bake the renderer around 1/4/6 views.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ16: Previous-frame Hi-Z. Keep.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ17: Make Hi-Z a renderer service, but keep per-system buffers separate.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ18: Keep GPU counters, but debug/profiling only. They must never add a synchronization point to the render path. Your delayed readback design already heads in the right direction.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ19: Split into scene_assets.c, scene_instances.c, scene_pass.c.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ20: Keep 2D and 3D separate implementations. Share conventions, not buffers.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ21: Game owns explicit dirty lists. Don't diff all instances every frame.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ22: Static maximum draw capacity. GPU writes the actual count.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ23: GPU tables are authoritative. CPU can keep lightweight asset metadata for editor/debug tooling, but don't maintain a second mutable rendering state.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ24: scene_create() can fail, uploads return bool, internal invariant violations assert.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ25: Absolutely no wait_idle in the frame loop. Resize/reallocation happens through an explicit resource-lifetime path.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ26: Clamp at capacity and increment dropped_commands. Never silently overflow.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ27: Start with global atomic compaction for simplicity, but keep the shader structured so subgroup/workgroup compaction can replace it without changing the API. Don't hard-code "64-thread subgroup" assumptions.[m
[32m+[m[32mQ28: visible[] representation remains implementation-private.[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mQ29: Rename it to:[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mscene_disable_occlusion(frames);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mA camera cut is an occlusion-history problem, not an LOD problem[m

[32m+[m[32m                                       (ByteSpan){updates, (uint32_t)(n * sizeof(InstanceUpdate))}))[m
[32m+[m[32m                p->uploaded = true;[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(updates);[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (p->skins_live)[m
[32m+[m[32m        scene_skin_update(p->scene, cmd, p->anim_time);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_cull(p->scene, cmd, 0);[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_draw(p->scene, cmd, color, depth, 0);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    /* Auto-capture periodically once warmup is over, so a headless probe can[m
[32m+[m[32m       compare several frames for flicker without anyone watching the window. */[m
[32m+[m[32m    static int32_t probe_shot = -1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (probe_shot < 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        probe_shot = 120;[m
[32m+[m[32m    else if (probe_shot > 0 && --probe_shot == 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        static int32_t probe_idx = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (probe_idx < 6) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            char path[64];[m
[32m+[m[32m            snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "capture_probe_%d.png", probe_idx++);[m
[32m+[m[32m            capture_take_screenshot(p->renderer, path);[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        probe_shot = 120;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
 }[m

[1mdiff --git a/shaders/hiz.slang b/shaders/hiz.slang[m

[32m+[m[32m// Hierarchical depth (Hi-Z) pyramid build, one level per dispatch.[m
[32m+[m[32m//[m
[32m+[m[32m// Reverse-Z puts the near plane at 1 and infinity at 0, so the conservative[m
[32m+[m[32m// "farthest surface in this tile" bound is the tile MIN. A tile touching[m
[32m+[m[32m// background (cleared depth 0) reduces to 0 and then never proves occlusion,[m
[32m+[m[32m// which is the safe direction. Each dispatch reads the previous level through a[m
[32m+[m[32m// plain texture fetch and writes one texel per 2x2 source texels.[m
[32m+[m[32m#include "common.slang"[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstruct HizPush {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint  src;      // bindless sampled index: scene depth for level 0, else level-1[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint  dst;      // bindless storage index of the level being written[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint2 src_size; // texels of the source level[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint2 dst_size; // texels of this level[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint2 pad;[m
[32m+[m[32m};[m
[32m+[m[32m[[vk::push_constant]] HizPush hiz;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m[shader("compute")][m
[32m+[m[32m[numthreads(8, 8, 1)][m
[32m+[m[32mvoid cs_hiz(uint3 tid : SV_DispatchThreadID) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (tid.x >= hiz.dst_size.x || tid.y >= hiz.dst_size.y) return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    int2 base = int2(tid.xy) * 2;[m
[32m+[m[32m    int2 last = int2(hiz.src_size) - 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    // 1.0 is the largest depth reverse-Z can store, so it is the identity for[m
[32m+[m[32m    // the min reduction.[m
[32m+[m[32m    float m = 1.0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    [unroll] for (int y = 0; y < 2; ++y) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        [unroll] for (int x = 0; x < 2; ++x) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            int2 s = min(base + int2(x, y), last);[m
[32m+[m[32m            m = min(m, textures[hiz.src].Load(int3(s, 0)).x);[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    storage_images[hiz.dst][int2(tid.xy)] = float4(m, m, m, 1.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m#include "../src/three_d/scene_shared.h"[m
[32m+[m[32m#include "common.slang"[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m#define MAX_MESHES_PER_SET 16[m
[32m+[m[32m#define MAX_COMMANDS 65536[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m/* Shader-side kill switch for the skin-deform block. Kept at 0 in the tree;[m
[32m+[m[32m   flip to 1 locally (or pass -DPETS_DEBUG_NOSKIN=1 to slangc) to force the[m
[32m+[m[32m   rigid VS path without touching the asset/palette uploads. */[m
[32m+[m[32m#ifndef PETS_DEBUG_NOSKIN[m
[32m+[m[32m#define PETS_DEBUG_NOSKIN 0[m
[32m+[m[32m#endif[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m[[vk::push_constant]] SceneViewPush pc;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat3 quat_to_basis(uint q, out float3x3 m) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float x = (float)((q >> 0) & 0x3FF) / 511.0 - 1.0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float y = (float)((q >> 10) & 0x3FF) / 511.0 - 1.0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float z = (float)((q >> 20) & 0x3FF) / 511.0 - 1.0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float w = (float)((q >> 30) & 0x3) / 1.5 - 1.0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float xx = x * x, yy = y * y, zz = z * z;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float xy = x * y, xz = x * z, yz = y * z;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float wx = w * x, wy = w * y, wz = w * z;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m[0][0] = 1 - 2 * (yy + zz); m[0][1] = 2 * (xy - wz);     m[0][2] = 2 * (xz + wy);[m
[32m+[m[32m    m[1][0] = 2 * (xy + wz);     m[1][1] = 1 - 2 * (xx + zz); m[1][2] = 2 * (yz - wx);[m
[32m+[m[32m    m[2][0] = 2 * (xz - wy);     m[2][1] = 2 * (yz + wx);     m[2][2] = 1 - 2 * (xx + yy);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return float3(x, y, z);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat3 instance_pos(SceneGpuInstance inst) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return float3(f16tof32(inst.pos_xy & 0xFFFF), f16tof32((inst.pos_xy >> 16) & 0xFFFF),[m
[32m+[m[32m                  f16tof32(inst.pos_z__scale & 0xFFFF));[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat instance_scale(SceneGpuInstance inst) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return f16tof32((inst.pos_z__scale >> 16) & 0xFFFF);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint instance_mesh_set(SceneGpuInstance inst) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return (inst.flags >> 24) & 0xFF;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat3 unpack_center(uint xy, uint zr) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return float3(f16tof32(xy & 65535), f16tof32((xy >> 16) & 65535), f16tof32(zr & 65535));[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mfloat unpack_radius(uint zr) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return f16tof32((zr >> 16) & 65535);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid clip_rows(out float4 r0, out float4 r1, out float4 r2, out float4 r3) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    r0 = float4(pc.view.rows[0][0], pc.view.rows[0][1], pc.view.rows[0][2], pc.view.rows[0][3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    r1 = float4(pc.view.rows[1][0], pc.view.rows[1][1], pc.view.rows[1][2], pc.view.rows[1][3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    r2 = float4(pc.view.rows[2][0], pc.view.rows[2][1], pc.view.rows[2][2], pc.view.rows[2][3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    r3 = float4(pc.view.rows[3][0], pc.view.rows[3][1], pc.view.rows[3][2], pc.view.rows[3][3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool sphere_outside_frustum(float3 c, float r, float4 r0, float4 r1, float4 r2, float4 r3) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float4 p = float4(c, 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cx = dot(r0, p);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cy = dot(r1, p);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cz = dot(r2, p);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cw = dot(r3, p);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float nx = length(r0.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float ny = length(r1.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float nz = length(r2.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float nw = length(r3.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cw + r * nw < 0.0) return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (abs(cx) > cw + r * nx) return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (abs(cy) > cw + r * ny) return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cz > cw + r * nz) return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cz < -r * nz) return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool hiz_occluded(float3 c, float r, float4 r0, float4 r1, float4 r2, float4 r3, uint hiz_base,[m
[32m+[m[32m                  uint hiz_levels, uint2 hiz_size) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (hiz_levels == 0) return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float4 center = float4(c, 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float nw = length(r3.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cw = dot(r3, center);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float w_near = cw - r * nw;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (w_near < 1e-3) return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float nz = length(r2.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float depth_max = (dot(r2, center) + r * nz) / w_near;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (depth_max >= 1.0 || depth_max <= 0.0) return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float nx = length(r0.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float ny = length(r1.xyz);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float inv_near = 1.0 / w_near;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float inv_far = 1.0 / (cw + r * nw);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 ndc_lo = float2(dot(r0, center) - r * nx, dot(r1, center) - r * ny) * inv_near;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 ndc_hi = float2(dot(r0, center) + r * nx, dot(r1, center) + r * ny) * inv_far;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (ndc_lo.x >= 1.0 || ndc_hi.x <= -1.0 || ndc_lo.y >= 1.0 || ndc_hi.y <= -1.0) return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 uv_lo = float2(ndc_lo.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - ndc_hi.y * 0.5);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 uv_hi = float2(ndc_hi.x * 0.5 + 0.5, 0.5 - ndc_lo.y * 0.5);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 level0 = float2(hiz_size);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float span = max((uv_hi.x - uv_lo.x) * level0.x, (uv_hi.y - uv_lo.y) * level0.y);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint level = (uint)clamp((int)ceil(log2(max(span, 1.0))), 0, (int)hiz_levels - 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 size = max(floor(level0 / exp2((float)level)), 1.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m    int2 lo = (int2)floor(uv_lo * size);[m
[32m+[m[32m    int2 hi = (int2)floor(uv_hi * size);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (hi.x - lo.x > 1 || hi.y - lo.y > 1) return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    int2 last = int2(size) - 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    lo = clamp(lo, int2(0, 0), last);[m
[32m+[m[32m    hi = clamp(hi, int2(0, 0), last);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint tex = hiz_base + level;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float m = min(min(textures[tex].Load(int3(lo, 0)).x, textures[tex].Load(int3(hi, 0)).x),[m
[32m+[m[32m                  min(textures[tex].Load(int3(lo.x, hi.y, 0)).x,[m
[32m+[m[32m                      textures[tex].Load(int3(hi.x, lo.y, 0)).x));[m
[32m+[m[32m    return m >= depth_max;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint select_lod(float dist, float scale, float lod_target, uint lod_group) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float threshold = dist * lod_target / scale;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint lod = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    while (lod + 1 < 4 && pc.view.lod_table[lod_group * 8 + lod + 1].error < threshold) ++lod;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return lod;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m[shader("compute")][m
[32m+[m[32m[numthreads(64, 1, 1)][m
[32m+[m[32mvoid cs_cull(uint3 tid : SV_DispatchThreadID) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint iid = tid.x / MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint local_m = tid.x % MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneGpuInstance inst = pc.view.instances[iid];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint set_id = instance_mesh_set(inst);[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneMeshSet set = pc.view.mesh_sets[set_id];[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (local_m >= set.count) return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint mesh_id = set.first + local_m;[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneGpuMesh mesh = pc.meshes[mesh_id];[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float scale = instance_scale(inst);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3x3 basis;[m
[32m+[m[32m    quat_to_basis(inst.quat, basis);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 local_center = unpack_center(mesh.local_center, mesh.local_radius);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float local_radius = unpack_radius(mesh.local_radius);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 world_center = mul(basis, scale * local_center) + instance_pos(inst);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float world_radius = local_radius * scale;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float4 r0, r1, r2, r3;[m
[32m+[m[32m    clip_rows(r0, r1, r2, r3);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (sphere_outside_frustum(world_center, world_radius, r0, r1, r2, r3)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        InterlockedAdd(pc.view.counters[0], 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint hiz_levels = (pc.misc >> 8) & 0xFF;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint hiz_base = pc.misc & 0xFFFF;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (hiz_levels != 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint2 hiz_size = uint2(pc.view.hiz_size[0], pc.view.hiz_size[1]);[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (hiz_occluded(world_center, world_radius, r0, r1, r2, r3, hiz_base, hiz_levels, hiz_size)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            InterlockedAdd(pc.view.counters[1], 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m            return;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float view_z = -(r3.x * world_center.x + r3.y * world_center.y + r3.z * world_center.z + r3.w);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float dist = max(view_z - world_radius, 0.001);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint lod = select_lod(dist, scale, pc.view.lod_target, mesh.lod_group);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint dci;[m
[32m+[m[32m    InterlockedAdd(pc.view.command_count[0], 1, dci);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (dci >= MAX_COMMANDS) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        InterlockedAdd(pc.view.counters[2], 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneLodRow lod_row = pc.view.lod_table[mesh.lod_group * 8 + lod];[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.draws[dci].index_count = lod_row.index_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.draws[dci].instance_count = 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.draws[dci].first_index = lod_row.first_index;[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.draws[dci].vertex_offset = lod_row.vertex_offset;[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.draws[dci].first_instance = dci;[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.draws[dci].draw_id = mesh_id;[m
[32m+[m[32m    pc.view.visible[dci] = (iid & 0xFFFF) | ((mesh_id & 0xFFFF) << 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m    InterlockedAdd(pc.view.counters[3], 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m    InterlockedAdd(pc.view.counters[4 + lod], 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstruct Varying {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float4 position : SV_Position;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 normal : TEXCOORD0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 color : COLOR0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 uv : TEXCOORD1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint mat : TEXCOORD2;[m
[32m+[m[32m};[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m[shader("vertex")][m
[32m+[m[32mVarying vs_main(uint vertex : SV_VertexID, uint instance : SV_InstanceID) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    // SV_InstanceID includes firstInstance, which is the compacted command index.[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint entry = pc.view.visible[instance];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint iid = entry & 0xFFFF;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint mesh_id = entry >> 16;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneGpuInstance inst = pc.view.instances[iid];[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneGpuMesh mesh = pc.meshes[mesh_id];[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float scale = instance_scale(inst);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3x3 basis;[m
[32m+[m[32m    quat_to_basis(inst.quat, basis);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 pos = instance_pos(inst);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    ScenePackedVertex packed = mesh.vertex_stream[vertex];[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 local = float3(f16tof32(packed.position_xy & 65535),[m
[32m+[m[32m                          f16tof32(packed.position_xy >> 16),[m
[32m+[m[32m                          f16tof32(packed.position_z & 65535));[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float2 oct = max(float2(int(packed.normal_oct << 16) >> 16, int(packed.normal_oct) >> 16) / 32767.0, -1.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 normal = float3(oct, 1 - abs(oct.x) - abs(oct.y));[m
[32m+[m[32m    float fold = max(-normal.z, 0.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m    normal.xy += float2(normal.x >= 0 ? -fold : fold, normal.y >= 0 ? -fold : fold);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneSkinRecord sr = pc.skin_table[mesh_id];[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (PETS_DEBUG_NOSKIN == 0 && uint64_t(sr.skin_stream) != 0ull) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint phase = iid & 7u;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint anim = (iid >> 3) & 1u;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint sw = vertex * 8u;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint4 j = uint4(sr.skin_stream[sw + 0u], sr.skin_stream[sw + 1u], sr.skin_stream[sw + 2u],[m
[32m+[m[32m                        sr.skin_stream[sw + 3u]);[m
[32m+[m[32m        float4 w = float4(sr.skin_stream[sw + 4u], sr.skin_stream[sw + 5u], sr.skin_stream[sw + 6u],[m
[32m+[m[32m                          sr.skin_stream[sw + 7u]) * (1.0 / 65535.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m        j = min(j, max(sr.joint_count, 1u) - 1u);[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint pbase = (anim * 8u + phase) * sr.joint_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m        /* CPU palettes are row-major (mesh_world * joint_world * inverse_bind),[m
[32m+[m[32m           Slang stores buffer float4x4 column-major, so the row-vector form[m
[32m+[m[32m           mul(v, m) applies the intended matrix; mul(m, v) would transpose it. */[m
[32m+[m[32m        float4 lp = float4(local, 1.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m        float4 sp = mul(lp, sr.palette[pbase + j.x].m) * w.x + mul(lp, sr.palette[pbase + j.y].m) * w.y +[m
[32m+[m[32m                    mul(lp, sr.palette[pbase + j.z].m) * w.z + mul(lp, sr.palette[pbase + j.w].m) * w.w;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local = sp.xyz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float3 sn = mul(normal, (float3x3)sr.palette[pbase + j.x].m) * w.x +[m
[32m+[m[32m                    mul(normal, (float3x3)sr.palette[pbase + j.y].m) * w.y +[m
[32m+[m[32m                    mul(normal, (float3x3)sr.palette[pbase + j.z].m) * w.z +[m
[32m+[m[32m                    mul(normal, (float3x3)sr.palette[pbase + j.w].m) * w.w;[m
[32m+[m[32m        normal = sn;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 world = mul(basis, scale * local) + pos;[m
[32m+[m[32m    normal = mul(basis, normal);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float4 r0, r1, r2, r3;[m
[32m+[m[32m    clip_rows(r0, r1, r2, r3);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    Varying output;[m
[32m+[m[32m    output.position = float4(dot(r0, float4(world, 1)),[m
[32m+[m[32m                             dot(r1, float4(world, 1)),[m
[32m+[m[32m                             dot(r2, float4(world, 1)),[m
[32m+[m[32m                             dot(r3, float4(world, 1)));[m
[32m+[m[32m    output.normal = normalize(normal);[m
[32m+[m[32m    output.color = float3(1, 1, 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m    output.uv = float2(f16tof32(packed.uv & 65535), f16tof32(packed.uv >> 16));[m
[32m+[m[32m    output.mat = (mesh.pack >> 20) & 0xFFF;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return output;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m[shader("fragment")][m
[32m+[m[32mfloat4 fs_main(Varying input) : SV_Target0 {[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneGpuMaterial mat = pc.view.materials[input.mat];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float4 base = float4(mat.base_color & 255, (mat.base_color >> 8) & 255,[m
[32m+[m[32m                         (mat.base_color >> 16) & 255, mat.base_color >> 24) / 255.0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint albedo = mat.texture & 0xFFFF;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 albedo_rgb = base.rgb;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (albedo != 0xFFFF)[m
[32m+[m[32m        albedo_rgb *= textures[albedo].Sample(samplers[pc.view.sampler], input.uv).rgb;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float3 sun_dir = float3(pc.view.sun[0], pc.view.sun[1], pc.view.sun[2]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float ambient = pc.view.sun[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float light = ambient + (1 - ambient) * max(dot(normalize(input.normal), normalize(sun_dir)), 0.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return float4(albedo_rgb * light, 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m        goto fail;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!scene_span_alloc(s, (VkDeviceSize)8 * sizeof(uint32_t), &s->counter_slice))[m
[32m+[m[32m        goto fail;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    forEach(i, MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!create_buffer(vk, 128, VK_BUFFER_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT, VMA_MEMORY_USAGE_CPU_ONLY,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           &s->readback[i]))[m
[32m+[m[32m            goto fail;[m
[32m+[m[32m        memset(s->readback[i].mapping, 0, 128);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < max_views; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!scene_span_alloc(s, (VkDeviceSize)SCENE_MAX_COMMANDS * sizeof(SceneGpuDraw), &s->command_slices[i]))[m
[32m+[m[32m            goto fail;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!scene_span_alloc(s, (VkDeviceSize)SCENE_MAX_COMMANDS * sizeof(uint32_t), &s->visible_slices[i]))[m
[32m+[m[32m            goto fail;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!scene_span_alloc(s, (VkDeviceSize)sizeof(uint32_t), &s->count_slices[i]))[m
[32m+[m[32m            goto fail;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!scene_span_alloc(s, (VkDeviceSize)sizeof(SceneGpuView), &s->view_slices[i]))[m
[32m+[m[32m            goto fail;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    {[m
[32m+[m[32m        GraphicsPipelineConfig cfg = pipeline_config_default();[m
[32m+[m[32m        cfg.vert_path = "compiledshaders/scene.vert.spv";[m
[32m+[m[32m        cfg.frag_path = "compiledshaders/scene.frag.spv";[m
[32m+[m[32m        cfg.color_attachment_count = 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m        cfg.color_formats = color_format;[m
[32m+[m[32m        cfg.depth_format = *depth_format;[m
[32m+[m[32m        cfg.blends[0] = blend_disabled();[m
[32m+[m[32m        s->pipeline_scene = pipeline_create_graphics(vk, &cfg);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->pipeline_cull = pipeline_create_compute(vk, "compiledshaders/scene.cs_cull.comp.spv");[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->pipeline_hiz = pipeline_create_compute(vk, "compiledshaders/hiz.cs_hiz.comp.spv");[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->occlusion_mode = OCCLUSION_HIZ_PREV_FRAME;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->lod_target = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->sampler = vk->default_samplers.samplers[SAMPLER_LINEAR_CLAMP];[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_assets_init(vk);[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_instances_init(max_instances);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    return s;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mfail:[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_destroy(s);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void cam_orbit(SceneCamera *cam, const Input *input, float dt) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float turn = 1.6f * dt;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (mouse_down(input, MOUSE_LEFT)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        cam->yaw   += (float)input->mouse_dx * 0.006f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        cam->pitch -= (float)input->mouse_dy * 0.006f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_LEFT) || key_down(input, KEY_A)) cam->yaw -= turn;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_RIGHT) || key_down(input, KEY_D)) cam->yaw += turn;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_UP) || key_down(input, KEY_W)) cam->pitch += turn;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_DOWN) || key_down(input, KEY_S)) cam->pitch -= turn;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_pressed(input, KEY_Q)) cam->third_dist *= 1.15f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_pressed(input, KEY_E)) cam->third_dist *= 0.87f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    cam->third_dist *= 1.0f - (float)input->scroll_y * 0.08f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->pitch > 1.5f) cam->pitch = 1.5f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->pitch < -1.5f) cam->pitch = -1.5f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->third_dist < 6.0f) cam->third_dist = 6.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->third_dist > 500.0f) cam->third_dist = 500.0f;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cp = cosf(cam->pitch), sp = sinf(cam->pitch);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float cy = cosf(cam->yaw), sy = sinf(cam->yaw);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cam->position[0] = cam->focus[0] + cp * sy * cam->third_dist;[m
[32m+[m[32m    cam->position[1] = cam->focus[1] + sp * cam->third_dist;[m
[32m+[m[32m    cam->position[2] = cam->focus[2] - cp * cy * cam->third_dist;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void cam_fly(SceneCamera *cam, const Input *input, float dt) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float turn = 1.6f * dt;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (mouse_down(input, MOUSE_LEFT)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        cam->yaw   += (float)input->mouse_dx * 0.006f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        cam->pitch -= (float)input->mouse_dy * 0.006f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->pitch > 1.5f) cam->pitch = 1.5f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->pitch < -1.5f) cam->pitch = -1.5f;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    vec3 fwd = {sinf(cam->yaw), 0.0f, -cosf(cam->yaw)};[m
[32m+[m[32m    vec3 right = {cosf(cam->yaw), 0.0f, sinf(cam->yaw)};[m
[32m+[m[32m    vec3 move = {0.0f};[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_W)) glm_vec3_add(move, fwd, move);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_S)) glm_vec3_sub(move, fwd, move);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_D)) glm_vec3_add(move, right, move);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_A)) glm_vec3_sub(move, right, move);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_SPACE) || key_down(input, KEY_E)) move[1] += 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (key_down(input, KEY_LEFT_SHIFT) || key_down(input, KEY_Q)) move[1] -= 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (glm_vec3_norm(move) > 0.001f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_normalize(move);[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_scale(move, cam->speed * dt, move);[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_add(cam->position, move, cam->position);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_camera_mode_update(SceneCamera *cam, CameraMode mode, const Input *input, float dt) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    cam->mode = mode;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (mode == CAM_FLY)[m
[32m+[m[32m        cam_fly(cam, input, dt);[m
[32m+[m[32m    else[m
[32m+[m[32m        cam_orbit(cam, input, dt);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_camera_update(SceneCamera *cam, float aspect) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    mat4 view, proj;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cam->mode == CAM_FLY) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 fwd = {cosf(cam->pitch) * sinf(cam->yaw), sinf(cam->pitch), -cosf(cam->pitch) * cosf(cam->yaw)};[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 center;[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_add(cam->position, fwd, center);[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 dir;[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_sub(center, cam->position, dir);[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 mid;[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_add(cam->position, dir, mid);[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 world_up = {0.0f, -1.0f, 0.0f};[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_lookat(cam->position, mid, world_up, view);[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 dir;[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_sub(cam->focus, cam->position, dir);[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 mid;[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_vec3_add(cam->position, dir, mid);[m
[32m+[m[32m        vec3 world_up = {0.0f, -1.0f, 0.0f};[m
[32m+[m[32m        glm_lookat(cam->position, mid, world_up, view);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    float f = 1.0f / tanf(cam->fov_y * 0.5f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(proj, 0, sizeof(proj));[m
[32m+[m[32m    proj[0][0] = f / aspect;[m
[32m+[m[32m    proj[1][1] = f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    proj[2][2] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    proj[2][3] = -1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    proj[3][2] = cam->near_z;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    glm_mat4_mul(proj, view, cam->view_proj);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            cam->clip_rows[i][j] = cam->view_proj[j][i];[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_destroy(Scene *scene) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!scene)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_hiz_destroy(scene);[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_assets_destroy(scene->vk);[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_instances_destroy();[m
[32m+[m[32m    scene_skins_destroy(scene->vk);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->instance_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->mesh_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->mesh_set_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->lod_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->skin_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->material_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    buffer_pool_free(scene->counter_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    forEach(i, MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT) destroy_buffer(scene->vk, &scene->readback[i]);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_VIEWS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        buffer_pool_free(scene->view_slices[i]);[m
[32m+[m[32m        buffer_pool_free(scene->command_slices[i]);[m
[32m+[m[32m        buffer_pool_free(scene->visible_slices[i]);[m
[32m+[m[32m        buffer_pool_free(scene->count_slices[i]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    free(scene);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic bool scene_span_alloc(Scene *s, VkDeviceSize bytes, BufferSlice *out) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    *out = buffer_pool_alloc(&s->vk->gpu_pool, bytes, 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return out->buffer != NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mVkDeviceAddress scene_slice_addr(const Scene *s, BufferSlice slice) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return s->vk->gpu_base_addr + slice.offset;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32mstatic inline uint32_t scene_pack_quat(float x, float y, float z, float w) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (x < -1.0f) x = -1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (x > 1.0f) x = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (y < -1.0f) y = -1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (y > 1.0f) y = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (z < -1.0f) z = -1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (z > 1.0f) z = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (w < -1.0f) w = -1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (w > 1.0f) w = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t qx = (uint32_t)lroundf((x + 1.0f) * 511.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t qy = (uint32_t)lroundf((y + 1.0f) * 511.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t qz = (uint32_t)lroundf((z + 1.0f) * 511.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t qw = (uint32_t)lroundf((w + 1.0f) * 1.5f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qx > 1023u) qx = 1023u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qy > 1023u) qy = 1023u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qz > 1023u) qz = 1023u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qw > 3u) qw = 3u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return qx | (qy << 10) | (qz << 20) | (qw << 30);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32mstatic void anim_quat_normalize(float q[4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float len = sqrtf(q[0] * q[0] + q[1] * q[1] + q[2] * q[2] + q[3] * q[3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (len > 1e-8f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        q[0] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        q[1] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        q[2] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        q[3] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        q[0] = q[1] = q[2] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        q[3] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void anim_quat_slerp(const float a[4], const float b[4], float t, float out[4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float dot = a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2] + a[3] * b[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float b1[4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (dot < 0.0f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        dot = -dot;[m
[32m+[m[32m        b1[0] = -b[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m        b1[1] = -b[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m        b1[2] = -b[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m        b1[3] = -b[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(b1, b, sizeof(b1));[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    float s0, s1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (dot > 0.9995f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        s0 = 1.0f - t;[m
[32m+[m[32m        s1 = t;[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float theta = acosf(dot);[m
[32m+[m[32m        float inv = 1.0f / sinf(theta);[m
[32m+[m[32m        s0 = sinf((1.0f - t) * theta) * inv;[m
[32m+[m[32m        s1 = sinf(t * theta) * inv;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0] = s0 * a[0] + s1 * b1[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1] = s0 * a[1] + s1 * b1[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2] = s0 * a[2] + s1 * b1[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[3] = s0 * a[3] + s1 * b1[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    anim_quat_normalize(out);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void anim_trs_to_mat(const float t[3], const float r[4], const float s[3], float out[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float qx = r[0], qy = r[1], qz = r[2], qw = r[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float xx = qx * qx, yy = qy * qy, zz = qz * qz;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float xy = qx * qy, xz = qx * qz, yz = qy * qz;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float wx = qw * qx, wy = qw * qy, wz = qw * qz;[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(out, 0, sizeof(float) * 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0][0] = (1.0f - 2.0f * (yy + zz)) * s[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0][1] = (2.0f * (xy - wz)) * s[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0][2] = (2.0f * (xz + wy)) * s[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1][0] = (2.0f * (xy + wz)) * s[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1][1] = (1.0f - 2.0f * (xx + zz)) * s[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1][2] = (2.0f * (yz - wx)) * s[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2][0] = (2.0f * (xz - wy)) * s[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2][1] = (2.0f * (yz + wx)) * s[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2][2] = (1.0f - 2.0f * (xx + yy)) * s[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0][3] = t[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1][3] = t[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2][3] = t[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[3][3] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void anim_mat_mul(const float a[4][4], const float b[4][4], float out[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float t[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            t[i][j] = a[i][0] * b[0][j] + a[i][1] * b[1][j] + a[i][2] * b[2][j] + a[i][3] * b[3][j];[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(out, t, sizeof(t));[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic uint32_t anim_key_span(const float *times, uint32_t count, float t) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (t <= times[0])[m
[32m+[m[32m        return 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (t >= times[count - 1])[m
[32m+[m[32m        return count - 2;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t lo = 0, hi = count - 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    while (hi - lo > 1) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t mid = (lo + hi) / 2;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (times[mid] <= t)[m
[32m+[m[32m            lo = mid;[m
[32m+[m[32m        else[m
[32m+[m[32m            hi = mid;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return lo;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void anim_sample_channel(const AnimChannel *ch, float t, float *out_vec, uint32_t *out_dim) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t dim = ch->path == 1 ? 4 : 3;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t stride = ch->interp == 2 ? dim * 3 : dim;[m
[32m+[m[32m    *out_dim = dim;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (ch->key_count == 1 || t <= ch->times[0]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t base = ch->interp == 2 ? dim : 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(out_vec, &ch->values[base], dim * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (t >= ch->times[ch->key_count - 1]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t base = (ch->key_count - 1) * stride + (ch->interp == 2 ? dim : 0);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(out_vec, &ch->values[base], dim * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (ch->interp == 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t k = anim_key_span(ch->times, ch->key_count, t);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(out_vec, &ch->values[k * stride], dim * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t k = anim_key_span(ch->times, ch->key_count, t);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float t0 = ch->times[k], t1 = ch->times[k + 1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float u = (t - t0) / (t1 - t0 > 1e-8f ? t1 - t0 : 1e-8f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (ch->interp == 1) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        const float *a = &ch->values[k * stride];[m
[32m+[m[32m        const float *b = &ch->values[(k + 1) * stride];[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (dim == 4) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            anim_quat_slerp(a, b, u, out_vec);[m
[32m+[m[32m        } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m            out_vec[0] = a[0] + (b[0] - a[0]) * u;[m
[32m+[m[32m            out_vec[1] = a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * u;[m
[32m+[m[32m            out_vec[2] = a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * u;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    float dt = t1 - t0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const float *p0 = &ch->values[k * stride + dim];[m
[32m+[m[32m    const float *m0 = &ch->values[k * stride + dim * 2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    const float *p1 = &ch->values[(k + 1) * stride + dim];[m
[32m+[m[32m    const float *m1 = &ch->values[(k + 1) * stride];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float u2 = u * u, u3 = u2 * u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float h00 = 2.0f * u3 - 3.0f * u2 + 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float h10 = u3 - 2.0f * u2 + u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float h01 = -2.0f * u3 + 3.0f * u2;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float h11 = u3 - u2;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < dim; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        out_vec[i] = h00 * p0[i] + h10 * dt * m0[i] + h01 * p1[i] + h11 * dt * m1[i];[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (dim == 4)[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_quat_normalize(out_vec);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_skin_register_from_gltf(VkBackend *vk, VkCommandBuffer cmd, cgltf_data *data,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                       cgltf_skin *skin, const float mesh_world[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t jcount = (uint32_t)skin->joints_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (jcount == 0 || jcount > 128 || !skin->inverse_bind_matrices)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t id = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_SKINS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!g_skins[i].used) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            id = i;[m
[32m+[m[32m            break;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (id == UINT32_MAX)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SkinReg *reg = &g_skins[id];[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(reg, 0, sizeof(*reg));[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->joints = (SkinJoint *)calloc(jcount, sizeof(SkinJoint));[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!reg->joints)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->joint_count = jcount;[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(reg->mesh_world, mesh_world, sizeof(reg->mesh_world));[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < jcount; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_node *node = skin->joints[j];[m
[32m+[m[32m        SkinJoint *sj = &reg->joints[j];[m
[32m+[m[32m        snprintf(sj->name, sizeof(sj->name), "%s", node->name ? node->name : "");[m
[32m+[m[32m        sj->parent = -1;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (node->parent) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < jcount; k++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (skin->joints[k] == node->parent) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    sj->parent = (int32_t)k;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    break;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (node->has_matrix) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            sj->bind_t[0] = node->matrix[12];[m
[32m+[m[32m            sj->bind_t[1] = node->matrix[13];[m
[32m+[m[32m            sj->bind_t[2] = node->matrix[14];[m
[32m+[m[32m            sj->bind_r[0] = sj->bind_r[1] = sj->bind_r[2] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m            sj->bind_r[3] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m            sj->bind_s[0] = sj->bind_s[1] = sj->bind_s[2] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(sj->bind_t, node->translation, sizeof(sj->bind_t));[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(sj->bind_r, node->rotation, sizeof(sj->bind_r));[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(sj->bind_s, node->scale, sizeof(sj->bind_s));[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        float ibm[16];[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_accessor_read_float(skin->inverse_bind_matrices, j, ibm, 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (int r = 0; r < 4; r++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (int c = 0; c < 4; c++)[m
[32m+[m[32m                sj->ibm[r * 4 + c] = ibm[c * 4 + r];[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)data;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float jw[4][4], sm[4][4], ibm_m[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->max_joint_dist = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < jcount; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        SkinJoint *sj = &reg->joints[j];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float local[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_trs_to_mat(sj->bind_t, sj->bind_r, sj->bind_s, local);[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (sj->parent >= 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            /* parent world recomputed on demand; joints may precede parents so resolve recursively */[m
[32m+[m[32m            float pw[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m            int32_t p = sj->parent;[m
[32m+[m[32m            float chain[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(chain, local, sizeof(chain));[m
[32m+[m[32m            while (p >= 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                SkinJoint *sp = &reg->joints[p];[m
[32m+[m[32m                float pl[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m                anim_trs_to_mat(sp->bind_t, sp->bind_r, sp->bind_s, pl);[m
[32m+[m[32m                float next[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m                anim_mat_mul(pl, chain, next);[m
[32m+[m[32m                memcpy(chain, next, sizeof(chain));[m
[32m+[m[32m                p = sp->parent;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(pw, chain, sizeof(pw));[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(jw, pw, sizeof(jw));[m
[32m+[m[32m        } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(jw, local, sizeof(jw));[m
[32m+[m[32m            reg->root_pos[0] = jw[0][3];[m
[32m+[m[32m            reg->root_pos[1] = jw[1][3];[m
[32m+[m[32m            reg->root_pos[2] = jw[2][3];[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        float dx = jw[0][3] - reg->root_pos[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float dy = jw[1][3] - reg->root_pos[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float dz = jw[2][3] - reg->root_pos[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float d = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (d > reg->max_joint_dist)[m
[32m+[m[32m            reg->max_joint_dist = d;[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(ibm_m, reg->joints[j].ibm, sizeof(ibm_m));[m
[32m+[m[32m        float ibm_mm[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(ibm_mm, ibm_m, sizeof(ibm_mm));[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_mat_mul(jw, ibm_mm, sm);[m
[32m+[m[32m        (void)sm;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkDeviceSize pal_bytes =[m
[32m+[m[32m        (VkDeviceSize)(2 * SCENE_SKIN_PHASES * jcount) * 16 * sizeof(float);[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->palette_slice = buffer_pool_alloc(&vk->gpu_pool, pal_bytes, 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->palette_cpu = (float *)malloc((size_t)pal_bytes);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!reg->palette_slice.buffer || !reg->palette_cpu) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (reg->palette_slice.buffer)[m
[32m+[m[32m            buffer_pool_free(reg->palette_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m        free(reg->joints);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memset(reg, 0, sizeof(*reg));[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t b = 0; b < 2 * SCENE_SKIN_PHASES; b++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        skin_bind_palette(reg, &reg->palette_cpu[(size_t)b * jcount * 16]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    ByteSpan span = {.data = reg->palette_cpu, .size = (uint32_t)pal_bytes};[m
[32m+[m[32m    renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, reg->palette_slice, span);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, reg->palette_slice.buffer, reg->palette_slice.offset, pal_bytes,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->clip_walk = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->clip_idle = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    reg->used = true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return id;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_skin_envelope(uint32_t skin_id, float *out_root, float *out_reach) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (skin_id >= SCENE_MAX_SKINS || !g_skins[skin_id].used) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        out_root[0] = out_root[1] = out_root[2] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        *out_reach = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(out_root, g_skins[skin_id].root_pos, 3 * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m    *out_reach = g_skins[skin_id].max_joint_dist;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint64_t scene_skin_palette_base(uint32_t skin_id, VkBackend *vk) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (skin_id >= SCENE_MAX_SKINS || !g_skins[skin_id].used)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return vk->gpu_base_addr + g_skins[skin_id].palette_slice.offset;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_skin_joint_count(uint32_t skin_id) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (skin_id >= SCENE_MAX_SKINS || !g_skins[skin_id].used)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return g_skins[skin_id].joint_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_anim_load(Scene *s, const char *path) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)s;[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_options options;[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(&options, 0, sizeof(options));[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_data *data = NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cgltf_parse_file(&options, path, &data) != cgltf_result_success || !data) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        log_error("[scene] anim parse failed: %s", path);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    /* Channel times/values live in the binary buffer; without this the[m
[32m+[m[32m       accessors read through a NULL buffer and leave malloc garbage. */[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cgltf_load_buffers(&options, data, path) != cgltf_result_success) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        log_error("[scene] anim buffer load failed: %s", path);[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_free(data);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (cgltf_size ai = 0; ai < data->animations_count; ai++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_animation *anim = &data->animations[ai];[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t id = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_CLIPS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (!g_clips[i].used) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                id = i;[m
[32m+[m[32m                break;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (id == UINT32_MAX)[m
[32m+[m[32m            break;[m
[32m+[m[32m        AnimClip *clip = &g_clips[id];[m
[32m+[m[32m        memset(clip, 0, sizeof(*clip));[m
[32m+[m[32m        snprintf(clip->name, sizeof(clip->name), "%s", anim->name ? anim->name : "");[m
[32m+[m[32m        clip->channels = (AnimChannel *)calloc(anim->channels_count, sizeof(AnimChannel));[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!clip->channels)[m
[32m+[m[32m            break;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float duration = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (cgltf_size ci = 0; ci < anim->channels_count; ci++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            cgltf_animation_channel *ch = &anim->channels[ci];[m
[32m+[m[32m            cgltf_animation_sampler *smp = ch->sampler;[m
[32m+[m[32m            AnimChannel *dst = &clip->channels[clip->channel_count];[m
[32m+[m[32m            snprintf(dst->joint_name, sizeof(dst->joint_name), "%s",[m
[32m+[m[32m                     ch->target_node && ch->target_node->name ? ch->target_node->name : "");[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (ch->target_path == cgltf_animation_path_type_translation)[m
[32m+[m[32m                dst->path = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            else if (ch->target_path == cgltf_animation_path_type_rotation)[m
[32m+[m[32m                dst->path = 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m            else if (ch->target_path == cgltf_animation_path_type_scale)[m
[32m+[m[32m                dst->path = 2;[m
[32m+[m[32m            else[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (smp->interpolation == cgltf_interpolation_type_step)[m
[32m+[m[32m                dst->interp = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            else if (smp->interpolation == cgltf_interpolation_type_cubic_spline)[m
[32m+[m[32m                dst->interp = 2;[m
[32m+[m[32m            else[m
[32m+[m[32m                dst->interp = 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t keys = (uint32_t)smp->input->count;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t dim = dst->path == 1 ? 4 : 3;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t stride = dst->interp == 2 ? dim * 3 : dim;[m
[32m+[m[32m            dst->times = (float *)malloc((size_t)keys * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m            dst->values = (float *)malloc((size_t)keys * stride * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (!dst->times || !dst->values) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(dst->times);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(dst->values);[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t k = 0; k < keys; k++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                cgltf_accessor_read_float(smp->input, k, &dst->times[k], 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m                cgltf_accessor_read_float(smp->output, k, &dst->values[(size_t)k * stride], stride);[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (dst->times[k] > duration)[m
[32m+[m[32m                    duration = dst->times[k];[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            dst->key_count = keys;[m
[32m+[m[32m            clip->channel_count++;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        clip->duration = duration;[m
[32m+[m[32m        clip->used = true;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (getenv("PETS_SKINDBG")) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            fprintf(stderr, "[anim] clip[%u] '%s' dur=%.3f channels=%u\n", id, clip->name,[m
[32m+[m[32m                    (double)clip->duration, clip->channel_count);[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t c = 0; c < clip->channel_count && c < 8; c++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                AnimChannel *ch = &clip->channels[c];[m
[32m+[m[32m                fprintf(stderr, "[anim]   ch%u '%s' path=%u interp=%u keys=%u t0=%.4f tN=%.4f v0=(%.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g)\n",[m
[32m+[m[32m                        c, ch->joint_name, ch->path, ch->interp, ch->key_count,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ch->key_count ? (double)ch->times[0] : -1.0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ch->key_count ? (double)ch->times[ch->key_count - 1] : -1.0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ch->key_count && ch->values ? (double)ch->values[0] : -1.0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ch->key_count && ch->values ? (double)ch->values[1] : -1.0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ch->key_count && ch->values ? (double)ch->values[2] : -1.0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ch->key_count && ch->values ? (double)ch->values[3] : -1.0);[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_free(data);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_anim_find(const char *name) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_CLIPS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (g_clips[i].used && strcmp(g_clips[i].name, name) == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m            return i;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_skin_play_set(Scene *s, uint32_t set_id, uint32_t walk_clip, uint32_t idle_clip) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)s;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t skin = scene_slot_skin_set(set_id);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (skin >= SCENE_MAX_SKINS || !g_skins[skin].used)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (walk_clip < SCENE_MAX_CLIPS && g_clips[walk_clip].used)[m
[32m+[m[32m        g_skins[skin].clip_walk = walk_clip;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (idle_clip < SCENE_MAX_CLIPS && g_clips[idle_clip].used)[m
[32m+[m[32m        g_skins[skin].clip_idle = idle_clip;[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_skins[skin].has_clips =[m
[32m+[m[32m        g_skins[skin].clip_walk != UINT32_MAX || g_skins[skin].clip_idle != UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic int32_t skin_resolve_joint(const SkinReg *reg, const char *name) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (strcmp(reg->joints[j].name, name) == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m            return (int32_t)j;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return -1;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void skin_joint_world(const SkinReg *reg, float local_mats[][4][4], int32_t j, float out[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    int32_t p = reg->joints[j].parent;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (p < 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(out, local_mats[j], sizeof(float) * 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float pw[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        skin_joint_world(reg, local_mats, p, pw);[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_mat_mul(pw, local_mats[j], out);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void skin_bind_palette(const SkinReg *reg, float *dst) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float local_mats[128][4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        SkinJoint *sj = &reg->joints[j];[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_trs_to_mat(sj->bind_t, sj->bind_r, sj->bind_s, local_mats[j]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float jw[4][4], sm[4][4], ibm[4][4], mw_sm[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        skin_joint_world(reg, local_mats, (int32_t)j, jw);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(ibm, reg->joints[j].ibm, sizeof(ibm));[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_mat_mul(jw, ibm, sm);[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_mat_mul(reg->mesh_world, sm, mw_sm);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(&dst[j * 16], mw_sm, sizeof(mw_sm));[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void scene_skin_sample_pose(SkinReg *reg, uint32_t clip_id, float time, float *out_palette) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    AnimClip *clip = &g_clips[clip_id];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float t = clip->duration > 0.0f ? fmodf(time, clip->duration) : 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    float local_mats[128][4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_trs_to_mat(reg->joints[j].bind_t, reg->joints[j].bind_r, reg->joints[j].bind_s,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        local_mats[j]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t c = 0; c < clip->channel_count; c++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        AnimChannel *ch = &clip->channels[c];[m
[32m+[m[32m        int32_t j = skin_resolve_joint(reg, ch->joint_name);[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (j < 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m            continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t dim = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float v[4] = {0, 0, 0, 1};[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_sample_channel(ch, t, v, &dim);[m
[32m+[m[32m        SkinJoint *sj = &reg->joints[j];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float tt[3], rr[4], ss[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(tt, sj->bind_t, sizeof(tt));[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(rr, sj->bind_r, sizeof(rr));[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(ss, sj->bind_s, sizeof(ss));[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (ch->path == 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (sj->parent < 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                memcpy(tt, sj->bind_t, sizeof(tt));[m
[32m+[m[32m            } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                memcpy(tt, v, sizeof(tt));[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m        } else if (ch->path == 1) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(rr, v, sizeof(rr));[m
[32m+[m[32m        } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m            memcpy(ss, v, sizeof(ss));[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_trs_to_mat(tt, rr, ss, local_mats[j]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float jw[4][4], sm[4][4], ibm[4][4], mw_sm[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        skin_joint_world(reg, local_mats, (int32_t)j, jw);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(ibm, reg->joints[j].ibm, sizeof(ibm));[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_mat_mul(jw, ibm, sm);[m
[32m+[m[32m        anim_mat_mul(reg->mesh_world, sm, mw_sm);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(&out_palette[j * 16], mw_sm, sizeof(mw_sm));[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_skin_update(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, float time) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_SKINS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        SkinReg *reg = &g_skins[i];[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!reg->used || !reg->has_clips)[m
[32m+[m[32m            continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float walk_dur = reg->clip_walk != UINT32_MAX ? g_clips[reg->clip_walk].duration : 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float idle_dur = reg->clip_idle != UINT32_MAX ? g_clips[reg->clip_idle].duration : 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (walk_dur <= 0.0f)[m
[32m+[m[32m            walk_dur = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (idle_dur <= 0.0f)[m
[32m+[m[32m            idle_dur = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (uint32_t b = 0; b < 2 * SCENE_SKIN_PHASES; b++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t anim = b / SCENE_SKIN_PHASES;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t phase = b % SCENE_SKIN_PHASES;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t clip = anim == 0 ? reg->clip_walk : reg->clip_idle;[m
[32m+[m[32m            float dur = anim == 0 ? walk_dur : idle_dur;[m
[32m+[m[32m            float *dst = &reg->palette_cpu[(size_t)b * reg->joint_count * 16];[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (clip != UINT32_MAX) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                float pt = time + (float)phase * dur / (float)SCENE_SKIN_PHASES;[m
[32m+[m[32m                scene_skin_sample_pose(reg, clip, pt, dst);[m
[32m+[m[32m            } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                skin_bind_palette(reg, dst);[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        ByteSpan span = {.data = reg->palette_cpu,[m
[32m+[m[32m                         .size = (uint32_t)(2 * SCENE_SKIN_PHASES * reg->joint_count * 16 *[m
[32m+[m[32m                                            sizeof(float))};[m
[32m+[m[32m        static uint32_t skindump_tick = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if ((skindump_tick++ % 120) == 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            const float *p0 = &reg->palette_cpu[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t     bad_count = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            /* row-major: translation at out[0][3],out[1][3],out[2][3] = 3,7,11 */[m
[32m+[m[32m            fprintf(stderr, "[skin] t=%.2f skin=%u joints=%u j0_t=(%.3f,%.3f,%.3f) j1_t=(%.3f,%.3f,%.3f) j1_r=(%.4f,%.4f,%.4f,%.4f)",[m
[32m+[m[32m                    time, i, reg->joint_count, p0[3], p0[7], p0[11], p0[16 + 3], p0[16 + 7], p0[16 + 11],[m
[32m+[m[32m                    p0[16 + 0], p0[16 + 1], p0[16 + 2], p0[16 + 5]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            float max_dev = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                const float *m = &p0[j * 16];[m
[32m+[m[32m                for (int k = 0; k < 16; k++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    float want = (k == 0 || k == 5 || k == 10 || k == 15) ? 1.0f : 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    float d = m[k] - want;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (d < 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m                        d = -d;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (d > max_dev)[m
[32m+[m[32m                        max_dev = d;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            fprintf(stderr, " dev=%.5f", (double)max_dev);[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t j = 0; j < reg->joint_count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                for (uint32_t k = 0; k < 16; k++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    float v = p0[j * 16 + k];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (!(v > -1e10f && v < 1e10f)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                        if (bad_count < 4)[m
[32m+[m[32m                            fprintf(stderr, " BAD j%u[%u]=%g", j, k, (double)v);[m
[32m+[m[32m                        bad_count++;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    }[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (bad_count > 4)[m
[32m+[m[32m                fprintf(stderr, " (+%u more)", bad_count - 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (skindump_tick == 1) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                static float bind_full[128 * 16];[m
[32m+[m[32m                skin_bind_palette(reg, bind_full);[m
[32m+[m[32m                const float *bind0 = bind_full;[m
[32m+[m[32m                fprintf(stderr, "[skin] mesh_world={%.6g %.6g %.6g %.6g | %.6g %.6g %.6g %.6g | "[m
[32m+[m[32m                                "%.6g %.6g %.6g %.6g | %.6g %.6g %.6g %.6g}\n",[m
[32m+[m[32m                        (double)reg->mesh_world[0][0], (double)reg->mesh_world[0][1], (double)reg->mesh_world[0][2],[m
[32m+[m[32m                        (double)reg->mesh_world[0][3], (double)reg->mesh_world[1][0], (double)reg->mesh_world[1][1],[m
[32m+[m[32m                        (double)reg->mesh_world[1][2], (double)reg->mesh_world[1][3], (double)reg->mesh_world[2][0],[m
[32m+[m[32m                        (double)reg->mesh_world[2][1], (double)reg->mesh_world[2][2], (double)reg->mesh_world[2][3],[m
[32m+[m[32m                        (double)reg->mesh_world[3][0], (double)reg->mesh_world[3][1], (double)reg->mesh_world[3][2],[m
[32m+[m[32m                        (double)reg->mesh_world[3][3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                for (uint32_t j = 0; j < (reg->joint_count > 2 ? 2u : reg->joint_count); j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    fprintf(stderr, "[skin] j%u bind_t=(%.6g %.6g %.6g) bind_r=(%.6g %.6g %.6g %.6g) "[m
[32m+[m[32m                                    "bind_s=(%.6g %.6g %.6g) parent=%d\n",[m
[32m+[m[32m                            j, (double)reg->joints[j].bind_t[0], (double)reg->joints[j].bind_t[1],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)reg->joints[j].bind_t[2], (double)reg->joints[j].bind_r[0],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)reg->joints[j].bind_r[1], (double)reg->joints[j].bind_r[2],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)reg->joints[j].bind_r[3], (double)reg->joints[j].bind_s[0],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)reg->joints[j].bind_s[1], (double)reg->joints[j].bind_s[2],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            reg->joints[j].parent);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    fprintf(stderr, "[skin] j%u walk m={%.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g | %.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g | %.5g %.5g "[m
[32m+[m[32m                                    "%.5g %.5g | %.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g}\n",[m
[32m+[m[32m                            j, (double)p0[j * 16 + 0], (double)p0[j * 16 + 1], (double)p0[j * 16 + 2],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)p0[j * 16 + 3], (double)p0[j * 16 + 4], (double)p0[j * 16 + 5],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)p0[j * 16 + 6], (double)p0[j * 16 + 7], (double)p0[j * 16 + 8],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)p0[j * 16 + 9], (double)p0[j * 16 + 10], (double)p0[j * 16 + 11],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)p0[j * 16 + 12], (double)p0[j * 16 + 13], (double)p0[j * 16 + 14],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)p0[j * 16 + 15]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    fprintf(stderr, "[skin] j%u bind m={%.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g | %.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g | %.5g %.5g "[m
[32m+[m[32m                                    "%.5g %.5g | %.5g %.5g %.5g %.5g}\n",[m
[32m+[m[32m                            j, (double)bind0[j * 16 + 0], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 1], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 2],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)bind0[j * 16 + 3], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 4], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 5],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)bind0[j * 16 + 6], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 7], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 8],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)bind0[j * 16 + 9], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 10], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 11],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)bind0[j * 16 + 12], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 13], (double)bind0[j * 16 + 14],[m
[32m+[m[32m                            (double)bind0[j * 16 + 15]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            fprintf(stderr, "\n");[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(s->vk, cmd, reg->palette_slice, span);[m
[32m+[m[32m        cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, reg->palette_slice.buffer, reg->palette_slice.offset,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           reg->palette_slice.size, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_skin_palette_slices(BufferSlice *out, uint32_t cap) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t n = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_SKINS && n < cap; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (g_skins[i].used && g_skins[i].palette_slice.buffer)[m
[32m+[m[32m            out[n++] = g_skins[i].palette_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return n;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_skins_destroy(VkBackend *vk) {    (void)vk;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_SKINS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        buffer_pool_free(g_skins[i].palette_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m        free(g_skins[i].joints);[m
[32m+[m[32m        free(g_skins[i].palette_cpu);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(g_skins, 0, sizeof(g_skins));[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_CLIPS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (uint32_t c = 0; c < g_clips[i].channel_count; c++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(g_clips[i].channels[c].times);[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(g_clips[i].channels[c].values);[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        free(g_clips[i].channels);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(g_clips, 0, sizeof(g_clips));[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_mesh_set_add(Scene *s, const MeshSetDesc *desc) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)s;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (g_assets.mesh_set_count >= SCENE_MAX_MESH_SETS)[m

[32m+[m[32m    if (!desc || !desc->mesh_slots || desc->mesh_count == 0)[m

[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t id = g_assets.mesh_set_count++;[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_assets.mesh_sets[id].first = desc->mesh_slots[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_assets.mesh_sets[id].count = desc->mesh_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return id;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m    return id;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
