[32m+[m[32mstatic uint32_t pack_half2(float a, float b) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    return (uint32_t)mu_quantize_half(a) | ((uint32_t)mu_quantize_half(b) << 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic uint32_t pack_oct16(float x, float y, float z) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float len = sqrtf(x * x + y * y + z * z);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (len > 0.0f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        x /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        y /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        z /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    float inv = 1.0f / (fabsf(x) + fabsf(y) + fabsf(z) + 1e-8f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    float ox = x * inv;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float oy = y * inv;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (z < 0.0f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float t = (1.0f - fabsf(oy)) * (ox >= 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m        float u = (1.0f - fabsf(ox)) * (oy >= 0.0f ? 1.0f : -1.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m        ox = t;[m
[32m+[m[32m        oy = u;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    int qx = (int)lroundf(ox * 32767.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    int qy = (int)lroundf(oy * 32767.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qx < -32768) qx = -32768;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qx > 32767) qx = 32767;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qy < -32768) qy = -32768;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (qy > 32767) qy = 32767;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return ((uint32_t)(qx & 0xFFFF)) | ((uint32_t)(qy & 0xFFFF) << 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void mat4_identity(float m[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(m, 0, sizeof(float) * 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m    m[0][0] = m[1][1] = m[2][2] = m[3][3] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void mat4_mul(const float a[4][4], const float b[4][4], float out[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float t[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            t[i][j] = a[i][0] * b[0][j] + a[i][1] * b[1][j] + a[i][2] * b[2][j] + a[i][3] * b[3][j];[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(out, t, sizeof(t));[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void node_world(const cgltf_node *node, float out[4][4]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float local[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (node->has_matrix) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(local, node->matrix, sizeof(local));[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float qx = node->rotation[0], qy = node->rotation[1], qz = node->rotation[2], qw = node->rotation[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float xx = qx * qx, yy = qy * qy, zz = qz * qz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float xy = qx * qy, xz = qx * qz, yz = qy * qz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float wx = qw * qx, wy = qw * qy, wz = qw * qz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float sx = node->scale[0], sy = node->scale[1], sz = node->scale[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m        mat4_identity(local);[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[0][0] = (1.0f - 2.0f * (yy + zz)) * sx;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[0][1] = (2.0f * (xy - wz)) * sy;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[0][2] = (2.0f * (xz + wy)) * sz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[1][0] = (2.0f * (xy + wz)) * sx;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[1][1] = (1.0f - 2.0f * (xx + zz)) * sy;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[1][2] = (2.0f * (yz - wx)) * sz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[2][0] = (2.0f * (xz - wy)) * sx;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[2][1] = (2.0f * (yz + wx)) * sy;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[2][2] = (1.0f - 2.0f * (xx + yy)) * sz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[0][3] = node->translation[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[1][3] = node->translation[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m        local[2][3] = node->translation[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (node->parent) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float parent[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        node_world(node->parent, parent);[m
[32m+[m[32m        mat4_mul(parent, local, out);[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(out, local, sizeof(local));[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void xform_point(const float m[4][4], const float p[3], float out[3]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0] = m[0][0] * p[0] + m[0][1] * p[1] + m[0][2] * p[2] + m[0][3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1] = m[1][0] * p[0] + m[1][1] * p[1] + m[1][2] * p[2] + m[1][3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2] = m[2][0] * p[0] + m[2][1] * p[1] + m[2][2] * p[2] + m[2][3];[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void xform_normal(const float m[4][4], const float n[3], float out[3]) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    float a00 = m[0][0], a01 = m[0][1], a02 = m[0][2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float a10 = m[1][0], a11 = m[1][1], a12 = m[1][2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float a20 = m[2][0], a21 = m[2][1], a22 = m[2][2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c00 = a11 * a22 - a12 * a21;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c01 = a12 * a20 - a10 * a22;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c02 = a10 * a21 - a11 * a20;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c10 = a02 * a21 - a01 * a22;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c11 = a00 * a22 - a02 * a20;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c12 = a01 * a20 - a00 * a21;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c20 = a01 * a12 - a02 * a11;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c21 = a02 * a10 - a00 * a12;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float c22 = a00 * a11 - a01 * a10;[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[0] = c00 * n[0] + c10 * n[1] + c20 * n[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[1] = c01 * n[0] + c11 * n[1] + c21 * n[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[2] = c02 * n[0] + c12 * n[1] + c22 * n[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    float len = sqrtf(out[0] * out[0] + out[1] * out[1] + out[2] * out[2]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (len > 1e-8f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        out[0] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        out[1] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m        out[2] /= len;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic const cgltf_accessor *prim_attr(const cgltf_primitive *prim, const char *name) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (cgltf_size i = 0; i < prim->attributes_count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (prim->attributes[i].type == cgltf_attribute_type_position && name[0] == 'P')[m
[32m+[m[32m            return prim->attributes[i].data;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (prim->attributes[i].type == cgltf_attribute_type_normal && name[0] == 'N')[m
[32m+[m[32m            return prim->attributes[i].data;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (prim->attributes[i].type == cgltf_attribute_type_texcoord && name[0] == 'T')[m
[32m+[m[32m            return prim->attributes[i].data;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (prim->attributes[i].type == cgltf_attribute_type_joints && name[0] == 'J')[m
[32m+[m[32m            return prim->attributes[i].data;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (prim->attributes[i].type == cgltf_attribute_type_weights && name[0] == 'W')[m
[32m+[m[32m            return prim->attributes[i].data;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic bool skin_bake_bind(cgltf_skin *skin, const cgltf_accessor *joints, const cgltf_accessor *weights,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           float *positions, float *normals, uint32_t vcount) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t jcount = (uint32_t)skin->joints_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (jcount == 0 || !skin->inverse_bind_matrices || jcount > 256)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    float *mats = (float *)malloc((size_t)jcount * 16 * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!mats)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t j = 0; j < jcount; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        float jw[4][4], sm[4][4], ibm[16];[m
[32m+[m[32m        node_world(skin->joints[j], jw);[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_accessor_read_float(skin->inverse_bind_matrices, j, ibm, 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m        float ibm_t[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (int r = 0; r < 4; r++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (int c = 0; c < 4; c++)[m
[32m+[m[32m                ibm_t[r][c] = ibm[c * 4 + r];[m
[32m+[m[32m        mat4_mul(jw, ibm_t, sm);[m
[32m+[m[32m        memcpy(&mats[j * 16], sm, sizeof(sm));[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vcount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t ji[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};[m
[32m+[m[32m        float w[4] = {1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_accessor_read_uint(joints, i, ji, 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_accessor_read_float(weights, i, w, 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m        float wsum = w[0] + w[1] + w[2] + w[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (wsum < 1e-6f) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            w[0] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m            wsum = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        float px = 0.0f, py = 0.0f, pz = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float nx = 0.0f, ny = 0.0f, nz = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m        float lx = positions[i * 3 + 0], ly = positions[i * 3 + 1], lz = positions[i * 3 + 2];[m
[32m+[m[32m        float lnx = normals[i * 3 + 0], lny = normals[i * 3 + 1], lnz = normals[i * 3 + 2];[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (w[k] == 0.0f || ji[k] >= jcount)[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            float wk = w[k] / wsum;[m
[32m+[m[32m            const float *m = &mats[ji[k] * 16];[m
[32m+[m[32m            px += wk * (m[0] * lx + m[1] * ly + m[2] * lz + m[3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            py += wk * (m[4] * lx + m[5] * ly + m[6] * lz + m[7]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            pz += wk * (m[8] * lx + m[9] * ly + m[10] * lz + m[11]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            nx += wk * (m[0] * lnx + m[1] * lny + m[2] * lnz);[m
[32m+[m[32m            ny += wk * (m[4] * lnx + m[5] * lny + m[6] * lnz);[m
[32m+[m[32m            nz += wk * (m[8] * lnx + m[9] * lny + m[10] * lnz);[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m        positions[i * 3 + 0] = px;[m
[32m+[m[32m        positions[i * 3 + 1] = py;[m
[32m+[m[32m        positions[i * 3 + 2] = pz;[m
[32m+[m[32m        normals[i * 3 + 0] = nx;[m
[32m+[m[32m        normals[i * 3 + 1] = ny;[m
[32m+[m[32m        normals[i * 3 + 2] = nz;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    free(mats);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void path_dir(const char *path, char *out, size_t cap) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    size_t n = strlen(path);[m
[32m+[m[32m    size_t cut = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (size_t i = 0; i < n; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (path[i] == '/' || path[i] == '\\')[m
[32m+[m[32m            cut = i + 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cut >= cap)[m
[32m+[m[32m        cut = cap - 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(out, path, cut);[m
[32m+[m[32m    out[cut] = '\0';[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic TextureID load_albedo(VkBackend *vk, cgltf_data *data, const char *path, cgltf_texture *tex) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!tex || !tex->image)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_image *img = tex->image;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    unsigned char *pixels = NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m    int w = 0, h = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (img->buffer_view) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_buffer_view *bv = img->buffer_view;[m
[32m+[m[32m        const unsigned char *base = (const unsigned char *)bv->buffer->data + bv->offset;[m
[32m+[m[32m        pixels = stbi_load_from_memory(base, (int)bv->size, &w, &h, NULL, 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else if (img->uri) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        char dir[1024];[m
[32m+[m[32m        char full[2048];[m
[32m+[m[32m        path_dir(path, dir, sizeof(dir));[m
[32m+[m[32m        snprintf(full, sizeof(full), "%s%s", dir, img->uri);[m
[32m+[m[32m        pixels = stbi_load(full, &w, &h, NULL, 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!pixels || w <= 0 || h <= 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (pixels)[m
[32m+[m[32m            stbi_image_free(pixels);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    TextureCreateDesc desc = {.width = (uint32_t)w,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .height = (uint32_t)h,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .depth = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .layers = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .mip_count = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .format = VK_FORMAT_R8G8B8A8_SRGB,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .usage = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_TRANSFER_DST_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .flags = 0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              .debug_name = "scene_albedo"};[m
[32m+[m[32m    TextureID id = create_texture(vk, &desc);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (id == UINT32_MAX) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        stbi_image_free(pixels);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkOffset3D offset = {0, 0, 0};[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkExtent3D extent = {(uint32_t)w, (uint32_t)h, 1};[m
[32m+[m[32m    ByteSpan span = {.data = pixels, .size = (uint32_t)(w * h * 4)};[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!texture_upload(vk, id, 0, 0, offset, extent, span)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        stbi_image_free(pixels);[m
[32m+[m[32m        destroy_texture(vk, id);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    stbi_image_free(pixels);[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)data;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return id;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_mesh_slot_add(Scene *s, const MeshSlotDesc *desc, uint32_t *out_slot) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)s;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (g_assets.mesh_count >= SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!desc)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t slot = g_assets.mesh_count++;[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneGpuMesh *m = &g_assets.meshes[slot];[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->vertex_stream = desc->vertex_stream;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->index_stream = g_assets.index_stream_addr;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->index_count = desc->index_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->pack = ((uint32_t)desc->base_vertex & 0xFFFFF) | ((desc->material & 0xFFF) << 20);[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->local_center = pack_half2(desc->center[0], desc->center[1]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->local_radius = pack_half2(desc->center[2], desc->radius);[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->lod_group = slot;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->flags = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->first_cluster = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    m->cluster_count = 0;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    LodGroup *lg = &g_assets.lod_groups[slot];[m
[32m+[m[32m    lg->count = desc->lod_count < SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS ? desc->lod_count : SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < lg->count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        lg->rows[i].index_count = desc->lods[i].index_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m        lg->rows[i].first_index = desc->lods[i].first_index;[m
[32m+[m[32m        lg->rows[i].vertex_offset = desc->lods[i].vertex_offset;[m
[32m+[m[32m        lg->rows[i].error = desc->lods[i].error;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = lg->count; i < SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        lg->rows[i] = lg->rows[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m        lg->rows[i].error = 1e30f;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    *out_slot = slot;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mSceneGpuMesh *scene_mesh_table(void) { return g_assets.meshes; }[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_mesh_count(void) { return g_assets.mesh_count; }[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_mesh_set_count(void) { return g_assets.mesh_set_count; }[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_slot_skin_set(uint32_t set_id) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (set_id >= g_assets.mesh_set_count)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t first = g_assets.mesh_sets[set_id].first;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (first >= SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return g_assets.slot_skin[first];[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_skin_stream_slices(BufferSlice *out, uint32_t cap) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t n = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < g_assets.skin_span_count && n < cap; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (g_assets.skin_spans[i].buffer)[m
[32m+[m[32m            out[n++] = g_assets.skin_spans[i];[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return n;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_vertex_stream_slices(BufferSlice *out, uint32_t cap) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t n = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < g_assets.vertex_slice_count && n < cap; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (g_assets.vertex_slices[i].buffer)[m
[32m+[m[32m            out[n++] = g_assets.vertex_slices[i];[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return n;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mSceneGpuMaterial *scene_material_table(void) { return g_assets.materials; }[m
[32m+[m[32mSceneMeshSet *scene_mesh_set_table(void) { return g_assets.mesh_sets; }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic bool upload_range(VkBackend *vk, VkCommandBuffer cmd, BufferSlice base, uint32_t first, uint32_t count,[m
[32m+[m[32m                         const void *src, uint32_t stride) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (count == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice slice = base;[m
[32m+[m[32m    slice.offset += (VkDeviceSize)first * stride;[m
[32m+[m[32m    slice.size = (VkDeviceSize)count * stride;[m
[32m+[m[32m    ByteSpan span = {.data = (const uint8_t *)src + (size_t)first * stride, .size = (uint32_t)count * stride};[m
[32m+[m[32m    return renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, slice, span);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32muint32_t scene_load_model(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, const char *path) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkBackend *vk = s->vk;[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_options options;[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(&options, 0, sizeof(options));[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_data *data = NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cgltf_parse_file(&options, path, &data) != cgltf_result_success || !data) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        log_error("[scene] parse failed: %s", path);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (cgltf_load_buffers(&options, data, path) != cgltf_result_success) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        log_error("[scene] buffers failed: %s", path);[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_free(data);[m
[32m+[m[32m        return UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t mesh_first = g_assets.mesh_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t mat_first = g_assets.material_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t set_first = g_assets.mesh_set_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t index_first = g_assets.index_used;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t slots[MAX_MESHES_PER_SET];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t slot_count = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t model_skin = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (cgltf_size mi = 0; mi < data->meshes_count; mi++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        cgltf_mesh *mesh = &data->meshes[mi];[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (cgltf_size pi = 0; pi < mesh->primitives_count; pi++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            cgltf_primitive *prim = &mesh->primitives[pi];[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (prim->type != cgltf_primitive_type_triangles || !prim->indices)[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            const cgltf_accessor *pos = prim_attr(prim, "POSITION");[m
[32m+[m[32m            const cgltf_accessor *nrm = prim_attr(prim, "NORMAL");[m
[32m+[m[32m            const cgltf_accessor *uv = prim_attr(prim, "TEXCOORD");[m
[32m+[m[32m            const cgltf_accessor *joints_acc = prim_attr(prim, "JOINTS");[m
[32m+[m[32m            const cgltf_accessor *weights_acc = prim_attr(prim, "WEIGHTS");[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (!pos || pos->count == 0 || pos->count > 65535)[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t vcount = (uint32_t)pos->count;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t icount = (uint32_t)prim->indices->count;[m
[32m+[m[32m            float *positions = (float *)malloc((size_t)vcount * 3 * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m            float *normals = (float *)malloc((size_t)vcount * 3 * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m            float *uvs = (float *)malloc((size_t)vcount * 2 * sizeof(float));[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t *indices = (uint32_t *)malloc((size_t)icount * sizeof(uint32_t));[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (!positions || !normals || !uvs || !indices) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            float world[4][4];[m
[32m+[m[32m            mat4_identity(world);[m
[32m+[m[32m            cgltf_skin *mesh_skin = NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (data->nodes_count > 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                for (cgltf_size ni = 0; ni < data->nodes_count; ni++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    cgltf_node *node = &data->nodes[ni];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (node->mesh == mesh) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                        node_world(node, world);[m
[32m+[m[32m                        mesh_skin = node->skin;[m
[32m+[m[32m                        break;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    }[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            float p[3], n[3], t[2], wp[3], wn[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m            float center[3] = {0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vcount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                cgltf_accessor_read_float(pos, i, p, 3);[m
[32m+[m[32m                positions[i * 3 + 0] = p[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                positions[i * 3 + 1] = p[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                positions[i * 3 + 2] = p[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (nrm) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    cgltf_accessor_read_float(nrm, i, n, 3);[m
[32m+[m[32m                } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    n[0] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    n[1] = 1.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    n[2] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                normals[i * 3 + 0] = n[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                normals[i * 3 + 1] = n[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                normals[i * 3 + 2] = n[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (uv) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    cgltf_accessor_read_float(uv, i, t, 2);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    uvs[i * 2 + 0] = t[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    uvs[i * 2 + 1] = t[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    uvs[i * 2 + 0] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    uvs[i * 2 + 1] = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            /* Skinned parts stay in bind-local space: the runtime palette[m
[32m+[m[32m               (mesh_world * joint_world * ibm) does the whole transform, so the[m
[32m+[m[32m               old static bake must not run here — applying both makes the VS[m
[32m+[m[32m               deform twice and geometry flies off-screen. */[m
[32m+[m[32m            bool part_skinned = mesh_skin && joints_acc && weights_acc;[m
[32m+[m[32m            (void)skin_bake_bind;[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (part_skinned && model_skin == UINT32_MAX)[m
[32m+[m[32m                model_skin = scene_skin_register_from_gltf(vk, cmd, data, mesh_skin, world);[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vcount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                p[0] = positions[i * 3 + 0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                p[1] = positions[i * 3 + 1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                p[2] = positions[i * 3 + 2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                n[0] = normals[i * 3 + 0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                n[1] = normals[i * 3 + 1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                n[2] = normals[i * 3 + 2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (!part_skinned) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    xform_point(world, p, wp);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    xform_normal(world, n, wn);[m
[32m+[m[32m                } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    /* Unbaked: the VS palette applies mesh_world itself. */[m
[32m+[m[32m                    wp[0] = p[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    wp[1] = p[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    wp[2] = p[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    wn[0] = n[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    wn[1] = n[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                    wn[2] = n[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                positions[i * 3 + 0] = wp[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                positions[i * 3 + 1] = wp[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                positions[i * 3 + 2] = wp[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                normals[i * 3 + 0] = wn[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                normals[i * 3 + 1] = wn[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                normals[i * 3 + 2] = wn[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                center[0] += wp[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                center[1] += wp[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                center[2] += wp[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            center[0] /= (float)vcount;[m
[32m+[m[32m            center[1] /= (float)vcount;[m
[32m+[m[32m            center[2] /= (float)vcount;[m
[32m+[m[32m            float radius = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vcount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                float dx = positions[i * 3 + 0] - center[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                float dy = positions[i * 3 + 1] - center[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                float dz = positions[i * 3 + 2] - center[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                float d = sqrtf(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz);[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (d > radius)[m
[32m+[m[32m                    radius = d;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (part_skinned && model_skin != UINT32_MAX) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                float env_root[3], env_reach = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                scene_skin_envelope(model_skin, env_root, &env_reach);[m
[32m+[m[32m                float eroot[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m                xform_point(world, env_root, eroot);[m
[32m+[m[32m                center[0] = eroot[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m                center[1] = eroot[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m                center[2] = eroot[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m                radius = env_reach + radius;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < icount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                uint32_t idx = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                cgltf_accessor_read_uint(prim->indices, i, &idx, 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m                indices[i] = idx;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t mat_id = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (prim->material) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                cgltf_material *mat = prim->material;[m
[32m+[m[32m                uint32_t base = 0xFFFFFFFFu;[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (mat->has_pbr_metallic_roughness) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    int r = (int)lroundf(mat->pbr_metallic_roughness.base_color_factor[0] * 255.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    int g = (int)lroundf(mat->pbr_metallic_roughness.base_color_factor[1] * 255.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    int b = (int)lroundf(mat->pbr_metallic_roughness.base_color_factor[2] * 255.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    int a = (int)lroundf(mat->pbr_metallic_roughness.base_color_factor[3] * 255.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (r < 0) r = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (r > 255) r = 255;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (g < 0) g = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (g > 255) g = 255;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (b < 0) b = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (b > 255) b = 255;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (a < 0) a = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (a > 255) a = 255;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    base = (uint32_t)r | ((uint32_t)g << 8) | ((uint32_t)b << 16) | ((uint32_t)a << 24);[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                uint32_t albedo = 0xFFFFu;[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (mat->has_pbr_metallic_roughness && mat->pbr_metallic_roughness.base_color_texture.texture) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    TextureID tex =[m
[32m+[m[32m                        load_albedo(vk, data, path, mat->pbr_metallic_roughness.base_color_texture.texture);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (tex != UINT32_MAX)[m
[32m+[m[32m                        albedo = tex & 0xFFFFu;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                MaterialDesc mdesc = {.base_color = base, .albedo_texture = albedo, .flags = 0};[m
[32m+[m[32m                mat_id = scene_material_add(s, &mdesc);[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            ScenePackedVertex *packed =[m
[32m+[m[32m                (ScenePackedVertex *)malloc((size_t)vcount * sizeof(ScenePackedVertex));[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (!packed) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vcount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                packed[i].position_xy = pack_half2(positions[i * 3 + 0], positions[i * 3 + 1]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                packed[i].position_z = pack_half2(positions[i * 3 + 2], 0.0f);[m
[32m+[m[32m                packed[i].normal_oct =[m
[32m+[m[32m                    pack_oct16(normals[i * 3 + 0], normals[i * 3 + 1], normals[i * 3 + 2]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                packed[i].uv = pack_half2(uvs[i * 2 + 0], uvs[i * 2 + 1]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            VkDeviceSize vbytes = (VkDeviceSize)vcount * sizeof(ScenePackedVertex);[m
[32m+[m[32m            BufferSlice vslice = buffer_pool_alloc(&vk->gpu_pool, vbytes, 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (!vslice.buffer) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(packed);[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            ByteSpan vspan = {.data = packed, .size = (uint32_t)vcount * (uint32_t)sizeof(ScenePackedVertex)};[m
[32m+[m[32m            renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, vslice, vspan);[m
[32m+[m[32m            cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, vslice.buffer, vslice.offset, vbytes,[m
[32m+[m[32m                               VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                               VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                               VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(packed);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (g_assets.vertex_slice_count >= SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                buffer_pool_free(vslice);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            g_assets.vertex_slices[g_assets.vertex_slice_count++] = vslice;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            BufferSlice skslice = {0};[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (part_skinned && model_skin != UINT32_MAX &&[m
[32m+[m[32m                g_assets.skin_span_count < SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                VkDeviceSize skbytes = (VkDeviceSize)vcount * 16;[m
[32m+[m[32m                skslice = buffer_pool_alloc(&vk->gpu_pool, skbytes, 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (skslice.buffer) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    uint8_t *skdata = (uint8_t *)malloc((size_t)skbytes);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (skdata) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vcount; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                            uint32_t ji[4] = {0, 0, 0, 0};[m
[32m+[m[32m                            float w[4] = {1.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f};[m
[32m+[m[32m                            cgltf_accessor_read_uint(joints_acc, i, ji, 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m                            cgltf_accessor_read_float(weights_acc, i, w, 4);[m
[32m+[m[32m                            uint16_t *d = (uint16_t *)(skdata + (size_t)i * 16);[m
[32m+[m[32m                            uint32_t jc = scene_skin_joint_count(model_skin);[m
[32m+[m[32m                            for (int k = 0; k < 4; k++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                                d[k] = (uint16_t)(ji[k] < jc ? ji[k] : 0);[m
[32m+[m[32m                                float q = w[k] < 0.0f ? 0.0f : (w[k] > 1.0f ? 1.0f : w[k]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                                d[4 + k] = (uint16_t)(q * 65535.0f + 0.5f);[m
[32m+[m[32m                            }[m
[32m+[m[32m                        }[m
[32m+[m[32m                        ByteSpan skspan = {.data = skdata, .size = (uint32_t)skbytes};[m
[32m+[m[32m                        renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, skslice, skspan);[m
[32m+[m[32m                        cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, skslice.buffer, skslice.offset, skbytes,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                           VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                           VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT |[m
[32m+[m[32m                                               VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                           VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m                        free(skdata);[m
[32m+[m[32m                        g_assets.skin_spans[g_assets.skin_span_count++] = skslice;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                        buffer_pool_free(skslice);[m
[32m+[m[32m                        memset(&skslice, 0, sizeof(skslice));[m
[32m+[m[32m                    }[m
[32m+[m[32m                } else if (skslice.buffer) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    buffer_pool_free(skslice);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    memset(&skslice, 0, sizeof(skslice));[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (part_skinned && model_skin != UINT32_MAX && !skslice.buffer) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    buffer_pool_free(vslice);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.vertex_slice_count--;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            static const float lod_ratios[SCENE_LOD_LEVELS] = {1.0f, 0.5f, 0.25f, 0.125f};[m
[32m+[m[32m            SceneLodRow lods[SCENE_LOD_LEVELS];[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t lod_count = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t prev_count = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t *scratch = (uint32_t *)malloc((size_t)icount * sizeof(uint32_t));[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t li = 0; li < SCENE_LOD_LEVELS; li++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                uint32_t want = (uint32_t)((float)icount * lod_ratios[li]);[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (want < 3)[m
[32m+[m[32m                    break;[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (li > 0 && (icount < 768 || want >= prev_count))[m
[32m+[m[32m                    break;[m
[32m+[m[32m                uint32_t got = icount;[m
[32m+[m[32m                float result_error = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (li > 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    got = (uint32_t)meshopt_simplify(scratch, indices, icount, positions, vcount, 12, want,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                                     1e-3f, 0, &result_error);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    if (got >= prev_count || got < 3)[m
[32m+[m[32m                        break;[m
[32m+[m[32m                } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    memcpy(scratch, indices, (size_t)icount * sizeof(uint32_t));[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (g_assets.index_used + got > SCENE_INDEX_POOL_MAX)[m
[32m+[m[32m                    break;[m
[32m+[m[32m                uint32_t first = g_assets.index_used;[m
[32m+[m[32m                for (uint32_t i = 0; i < got; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.index_mirror[first + i] = (uint16_t)scratch[i];[m
[32m+[m[32m                g_assets.index_used += got;[m
[32m+[m[32m                lods[lod_count].index_count = got;[m
[32m+[m[32m                lods[lod_count].first_index = first;[m
[32m+[m[32m                lods[lod_count].vertex_offset = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                lods[lod_count].error = li == 0 ? 0.0f : result_error;[m
[32m+[m[32m                lod_count++;[m
[32m+[m[32m                prev_count = got;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(scratch);[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (lod_count == 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m                free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m                continue;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            MeshSlotDesc sdesc = {.vertex_stream = vk->gpu_base_addr + vslice.offset,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .index_stream = g_assets.index_stream_addr,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .index_count = lods[0].index_count,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .base_vertex = 0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .material = mat_id,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .center = {center[0], center[1], center[2]},[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .radius = radius,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .lod_count = lod_count,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                  .lods = lods};[m
[32m+[m[32m            uint32_t slot = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (scene_mesh_slot_add(s, &sdesc, &slot) && slot_count < MAX_MESHES_PER_SET) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                slots[slot_count++] = slot;[m
[32m+[m[32m                if (part_skinned && model_skin != UINT32_MAX && skslice.buffer) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.slot_skin[slot] = model_skin;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.skin_mirror[slot].skin_stream = vk->gpu_base_addr + skslice.offset;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.skin_mirror[slot].palette = scene_skin_palette_base(model_skin, vk);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.skin_mirror[slot].joint_count = scene_skin_joint_count(model_skin);[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_assets.skin_mirror[slot].flags = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(positions);[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(normals);[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(uvs);[m
[32m+[m[32m            free(indices);[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t set_id = UINT32_MAX;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (slot_count > 0) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        MeshSetDesc setdesc = {.mesh_slots = slots, .mesh_count = slot_count};[m
[32m+[m[32m        set_id = scene_mesh_set_add(s, &setdesc);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    upload_range(vk, cmd, s->mesh_slice, mesh_first, g_assets.mesh_count - mesh_first, g_assets.meshes,[m
[32m+[m[32m                 (uint32_t)sizeof(SceneGpuMesh));[m
[32m+[m[32m    upload_range(vk, cmd, s->material_slice, mat_first, g_assets.material_count - mat_first,[m
[32m+[m[32m                 g_assets.materials, (uint32_t)sizeof(SceneGpuMaterial));[m
[32m+[m[32m    upload_range(vk, cmd, s->mesh_set_slice, set_first, g_assets.mesh_set_count - set_first,[m
[32m+[m[32m                 g_assets.mesh_sets, (uint32_t)sizeof(SceneMeshSet));[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = mesh_first; i < g_assets.mesh_count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        LodGroup *lg = &g_assets.lod_groups[i];[m
[32m+[m[32m        BufferSlice slice = s->lod_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m        slice.offset += (VkDeviceSize)i * SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS * sizeof(SceneLodRow);[m
[32m+[m[32m        slice.size = (VkDeviceSize)SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS * sizeof(SceneLodRow);[m
[32m+[m[32m        ByteSpan span = {.data = lg->rows,[m
[32m+[m[32m                         .size = SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS * (uint32_t)sizeof(SceneLodRow)};[m
[32m+[m[32m        renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, slice, span);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (g_assets.mesh_count > mesh_first) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        BufferSlice slice = s->skin_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m        slice.offset += (VkDeviceSize)mesh_first * sizeof(SceneSkinRecord);[m
[32m+[m[32m        slice.size = (VkDeviceSize)(g_assets.mesh_count - mesh_first) * sizeof(SceneSkinRecord);[m
[32m+[m[32m        ByteSpan span = {.data = (const uint8_t *)&g_assets.skin_mirror[mesh_first],[m
[32m+[m[32m                         .size = (g_assets.mesh_count - mesh_first) * (uint32_t)sizeof(SceneSkinRecord)};[m
[32m+[m[32m        renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, slice, span);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (g_assets.index_used > index_first) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        BufferSlice slice = g_assets.index_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m        slice.offset += (VkDeviceSize)index_first * sizeof(uint16_t);[m
[32m+[m[32m        slice.size = (VkDeviceSize)(g_assets.index_used - index_first) * sizeof(uint16_t);[m
[32m+[m[32m        ByteSpan span = {.data = &g_assets.index_mirror[index_first],[m
[32m+[m[32m                         .size = (g_assets.index_used - index_first) * (uint32_t)sizeof(uint16_t)};[m
[32m+[m[32m        renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(vk, cmd, slice, span);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    cgltf_free(data);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return set_id;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m#include "scene.h"[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m#include <stdlib.h>[m
[32m+[m[32m#include <string.h>[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct InstanceSlot {[m
[32m+[m[32m    bool     used;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t generation;[m
[32m+[m[32m} InstanceSlot;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic InstanceSlot *g_slots;[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic uint32_t     g_capacity;[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic uint32_t     g_allocated;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_instances_init(uint32_t capacity) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_capacity = capacity;[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_slots = (InstanceSlot *)calloc(capacity, sizeof(InstanceSlot));[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_allocated = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_instances_destroy(void) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    free(g_slots);[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_slots = NULL;[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_capacity = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    g_allocated = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_instance_reserve(Scene *s, uint32_t count, uint32_t *out_first) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)s;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (count == 0 || !out_first || g_allocated + count > g_capacity)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t first = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t found = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < g_capacity; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!g_slots[i].used) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (found == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m                first = i;[m
[32m+[m[32m            found++;[m
[32m+[m[32m            if (found == count) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                for (uint32_t j = 0; j < count; j++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_slots[first + j].used = true;[m
[32m+[m[32m                    g_slots[first + j].generation++;[m
[32m+[m[32m                }[m
[32m+[m[32m                g_allocated += count;[m
[32m+[m[32m                *out_first = first;[m
[32m+[m[32m                return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m            }[m
[32m+[m[32m        } else {[m
[32m+[m[32m            found = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_upload_instances(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, ByteSpan updates) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    const InstanceUpdate *u = (const InstanceUpdate *)updates.data;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t count = updates.size / (uint32_t)sizeof(InstanceUpdate);[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!u || count == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return true;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (u[i].slot >= g_capacity)[m
[32m+[m[32m            return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m        BufferSlice dst = s->instance_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m        dst.offset += (VkDeviceSize)u[i].slot * sizeof(SceneGpuInstance);[m
[32m+[m[32m        dst.size = sizeof(SceneGpuInstance);[m
[32m+[m[32m        ByteSpan src = {.data = &u[i].data, .size = (uint32_t)sizeof(SceneGpuInstance)};[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(s->vk, cmd, dst, src))[m
[32m+[m[32m            return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m        cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, dst.buffer, dst.offset, dst.size,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t end = u[i].slot + 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (end > s->instance_count)[m
[32m+[m[32m            s->instance_count = end;[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (end > s->instance_total)[m
[32m+[m[32m            s->instance_total = end;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->last_submitted = s->instance_count;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->instance_upload_pending = true;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_instance_free(Scene *s, uint32_t first, uint32_t count) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    (void)s;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (first + i < g_capacity && g_slots[first + i].used) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            g_slots[first + i].used = false;[m
[32m+[m[32m            g_allocated--;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m#include "scene.h"[m
[32m+[m[32m#include "scene_internal.h"[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m#include <stdlib.h>[m
[32m+[m[32m#include <string.h>[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic VkDeviceSize scene_hiz_dim(uint32_t src, uint32_t level) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t d = src >> (level + 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m    return d ? d : 1u;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic bool scene_hiz_create(Scene *s, uint32_t src_w, uint32_t src_h) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t levels = 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m    while (levels < SCENE_HIZ_MAX_LEVELS &&[m
[32m+[m[32m           (scene_hiz_dim(src_w, levels - 1) != 1 || scene_hiz_dim(src_h, levels - 1) != 1))[m
[32m+[m[32m        levels++;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < levels; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        RenderTargetSpec spec = {.width      = scene_hiz_dim(src_w, i),[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .height     = scene_hiz_dim(src_h, i),[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .layers     = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .format     = VK_FORMAT_R32_SFLOAT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .usage      = VK_IMAGE_USAGE_SAMPLED_BIT | VK_IMAGE_USAGE_STORAGE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .aspect     = VK_IMAGE_ASPECT_COLOR_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .mip_count  = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 .debug_name = "scene_hiz"};[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (!rt_create(s->vk, &s->hiz[i], &spec)) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t j = 0; j < i; j++)[m
[32m+[m[32m                rt_destroy(s->vk, &s->hiz[j]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            s->hiz_levels = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 1; i < levels; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (s->hiz[i].bindless_index != s->hiz[0].bindless_index + i) {[m
[32m+[m[32m            for (uint32_t j = 0; j < levels; j++)[m
[32m+[m[32m                rt_destroy(s->vk, &s->hiz[j]);[m
[32m+[m[32m            s->hiz_levels = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m            return false;[m
[32m+[m[32m        }[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_levels = levels;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_src_w  = src_w;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_src_h  = src_h;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_warmup = 2;[m
[32m+[m[32m    return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_hiz_destroy(Scene *s) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < s->hiz_levels; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        rt_destroy(s->vk, &s->hiz[i]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_levels = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_src_w  = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_src_h  = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mtypedef struct RetiredHiz {[m
[32m+[m[32m    RenderTarget targets[SCENE_HIZ_MAX_LEVELS];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t     levels;[m
[32m+[m[32m} RetiredHiz;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void scene_hiz_retire_free(VkBackend *vk, void *user) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    RetiredHiz *retired = (RetiredHiz *)user;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < retired->levels; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m        rt_destroy(vk, &retired->targets[i]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    free(retired);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mstatic void scene_hiz_retire(Scene *s) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (s->hiz_levels == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    RetiredHiz *retired = (RetiredHiz *)malloc(sizeof(RetiredHiz));[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(retired->targets, s->hiz, sizeof(s->hiz));[m
[32m+[m[32m    retired->levels = s->hiz_levels;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_levels   = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_src_w    = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->hiz_src_h    = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    memset(s->hiz, 0, sizeof(s->hiz));[m
[32m+[m[32m    delete_queue_defer(s->vk, s->vk->timeline_last_submitted, scene_hiz_retire_free, retired);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_hiz_build(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, RenderTarget *src, uint32_t src_index, uint32_t level) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    RenderTarget *dst = &s->hiz[level];[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    rt_transition_all(s->vk, cmd, src, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                      VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_SAMPLED_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    rt_transition_all(s->vk, cmd, dst, VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_GENERAL, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                      VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    flush_barriers(s->vk, cmd);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdBindPipeline(cmd, VK_PIPELINE_BIND_POINT_COMPUTE, s->vk->render_pipelines.pipelines[s->pipeline_hiz - 1]);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    struct HizPush {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t src;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t dst;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t src_w, src_h;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t dst_w, dst_h;[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t pad[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    } push = {.src   = src_index,[m
[32m+[m[32m              .dst   = dst->bindless_index,[m
[32m+[m[32m              .src_w = src->width,[m
[32m+[m[32m              .src_h = src->height,[m
[32m+[m[32m              .dst_w = dst->width,[m
[32m+[m[32m              .dst_h = dst->height};[m
[32m+[m[32m    dispatch_push(s->vk, cmd, BYTE_SPAN(push), (dst->width + 7u) / 8u, (dst->height + 7u) / 8u, 1);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_cull(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, uint32_t view) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (view >= s->view_count)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    bool hiz_on = s->occlusion_mode == OCCLUSION_HIZ_PREV_FRAME && s->hiz_warmup == 0 && s->hiz_disable_frames == 0 &&[m
[32m+[m[32m                  s->hiz_levels > 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t hiz_levels = hiz_on ? s->hiz_levels : 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t hiz_base   = hiz_on ? s->hiz[0].bindless_index : 0;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t mesh_count     = scene_mesh_count();[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t instance_count = s->instance_total;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (mesh_count == 0 || instance_count == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(s->vk, cmd, s->view_slices[view],[m
[32m+[m[32m                                    (ByteSpan){&s->views[view], sizeof(SceneGpuView)});[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->view_slices[view].buffer, s->view_slices[view].offset,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       sizeof(SceneGpuView), VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t total_threads = instance_count * MAX_MESHES_PER_SET;[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t groups        = (total_threads + 63u) / 64u;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneViewPush push = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        .view       = scene_slice_addr(s, s->view_slices[view]),[m
[32m+[m[32m        .meshes     = scene_slice_addr(s, s->mesh_slice),[m
[32m+[m[32m        .skin_table = scene_slice_addr(s, s->skin_slice),[m
[32m+[m[32m        .misc       = (hiz_base & 0xFFFF) | ((hiz_levels & 0xFF) << 8),[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice inst_read = s->instance_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    inst_read.size        = (VkDeviceSize)instance_count * sizeof(SceneGpuInstance);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice mesh_read = s->mesh_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    mesh_read.size        = (VkDeviceSize)mesh_count * sizeof(SceneGpuMesh);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice set_read  = s->mesh_set_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    set_read.size         = (VkDeviceSize)scene_mesh_set_count() * sizeof(SceneMeshSet);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice lod_read  = s->lod_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    lod_read.size         = (VkDeviceSize)mesh_count * SCENE_MAX_LOD_LEVELS * sizeof(SceneLodRow);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice cmd_write = s->command_slices[view];[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice vis_write = s->visible_slices[view];[m
[32m+[m[32m    vis_write.size        = (VkDeviceSize)SCENE_MAX_COMMANDS * sizeof(uint32_t);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice cnt_write = s->count_slices[view];[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice ctr_write = s->counter_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    ctr_write.size        = 4 * sizeof(uint32_t);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferAccess reads[] = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        {inst_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {mesh_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {set_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {lod_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {s->view_slices[view], VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferAccess writes[] = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        {cmd_write, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m         VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {vis_write, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {cnt_write, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m         VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {ctr_write, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m         VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    begin_pass(s->vk, cmd,[m
[32m+[m[32m               &(PassDesc){.color_count     = 0,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .pipeline        = s->pipeline_cull,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .push            = BYTE_SPAN(push),[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .buf_reads       = reads,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .buf_read_count  = 5,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .buf_writes      = writes,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .buf_write_count = 4});[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdDispatch(cmd, groups, 1, 1);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->counter_slice.buffer, s->counter_slice.offset, 8 * sizeof(uint32_t),[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->command_slices[view].buffer, s->command_slices[view].offset, 2 * sizeof(SceneGpuDraw),[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->visible_slices[view].buffer, s->visible_slices[view].offset, 8 * sizeof(uint32_t),[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t     slot       = s->vk->current_frame % MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT;[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkBufferCopy regions[3] = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        {.srcOffset = s->counter_slice.offset, .dstOffset = 0, .size = 8 * sizeof(uint32_t)},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {.srcOffset = s->command_slices[view].offset, .dstOffset = 32, .size = 2 * sizeof(SceneGpuDraw)},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {.srcOffset = s->visible_slices[view].offset, .dstOffset = 96, .size = 8 * sizeof(uint32_t)},[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdCopyBuffer(cmd, s->counter_slice.buffer, s->readback[slot].buffer, 1, &regions[0]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdCopyBuffer(cmd, s->command_slices[view].buffer, s->readback[slot].buffer, 1, &regions[1]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdCopyBuffer(cmd, s->visible_slices[view].buffer, s->readback[slot].buffer, 1, &regions[2]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->count_slices[view].buffer, s->count_slices[view].offset, sizeof(uint32_t),[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_DRAW_INDIRECT_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_INDIRECT_COMMAND_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->command_slices[view].buffer, s->command_slices[view].offset,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       s->command_slices[view].size, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_DRAW_INDIRECT_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_INDIRECT_COMMAND_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->visible_slices[view].buffer, s->visible_slices[view].offset,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       s->visible_slices[view].size, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_WRITE_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_buffer_barrier(cmd, s->readback[slot].buffer, 0, 128, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                       VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_HOST_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_HOST_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_draw(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd, RenderTarget *color, RenderTarget *depth, uint32_t view) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (view >= s->view_count)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (scene_mesh_count() == 0 || s->instance_total == 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (s->occlusion_mode == OCCLUSION_OFF) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        scene_hiz_retire(s);[m
[32m+[m[32m    } else if (s->hiz_levels == 0 || s->hiz_src_w != depth->width || s->hiz_src_h != depth->height) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        scene_hiz_retire(s);[m
[32m+[m[32m        scene_hiz_create(s, depth->width, depth->height);[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    forEach(v, SCENE_MAX_VIEWS) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        s->views[v].hiz_size[0] = s->hiz_levels ? s->hiz[0].width : 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        s->views[v].hiz_size[1] = s->hiz_levels ? s->hiz[0].height : 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    PassAttachment color_a = {.target = color, .load = LOAD_KEEP, .store = STORE_KEEP};[m
[32m+[m[32m    PassAttachment depth_a = {.target = depth, .load = LOAD_CLEAR, .store = STORE_KEEP};[m
[32m+[m[32m    depth_a.clear[0]       = 0.0f;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    SceneViewPush push = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        .view       = scene_slice_addr(s, s->view_slices[view]),[m
[32m+[m[32m        .meshes     = scene_slice_addr(s, s->mesh_slice),[m
[32m+[m[32m        .skin_table = scene_slice_addr(s, s->skin_slice),[m
[32m+[m[32m        .misc       = 0,[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice index_pool = scene_index_pool();[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice mesh_read  = s->mesh_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    mesh_read.size         = (VkDeviceSize)scene_mesh_count() * sizeof(SceneGpuMesh);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice skin_table_read = s->skin_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    skin_table_read.size        = (VkDeviceSize)scene_mesh_count() * sizeof(SceneSkinRecord);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice inst_read  = s->instance_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    inst_read.size         = (VkDeviceSize)s->instance_total * sizeof(SceneGpuInstance);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice mat_read   = s->material_slice;[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice vis_read   = s->visible_slices[view];[m
[32m+[m[32m    vis_read.size          = (VkDeviceSize)SCENE_MAX_COMMANDS * sizeof(uint32_t);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice  pal_read[8];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t     pal_count = scene_skin_palette_slices(pal_read, 8);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice skin_read[SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t skin_count = scene_skin_stream_slices(skin_read, SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice vertex_read[SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS];[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t vertex_count = scene_vertex_stream_slices(vertex_read, SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS);[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferAccess reads[8 + 8 + SCENE_MAX_MESH_SLOTS * 2] = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        {s->command_slices[view], VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_DRAW_INDIRECT_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_INDIRECT_COMMAND_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {s->count_slices[view], VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_DRAW_INDIRECT_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_INDIRECT_COMMAND_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {vis_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {inst_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {mesh_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {skin_table_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {index_pool, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_INDEX_INPUT_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_INDEX_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m        {mat_read, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_FRAGMENT_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT},[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t read_count = 8;[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < pal_count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        reads[read_count++] =[m
[32m+[m[32m            (BufferAccess){pal_read[i], VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT};[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < skin_count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        reads[read_count++] =[m
[32m+[m[32m            (BufferAccess){skin_read[i], VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT};[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < vertex_count; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        reads[read_count++] =[m
[32m+[m[32m            (BufferAccess){vertex_read[i], VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_STORAGE_READ_BIT};[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    begin_pass(s->vk, cmd,[m
[32m+[m[32m               &(PassDesc){.colors         = &color_a,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .color_count    = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .depth          = &depth_a,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .pipeline       = s->pipeline_scene,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .push           = BYTE_SPAN(push),[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .buf_reads      = reads,[m
[32m+[m[32m                           .buf_read_count = read_count});[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdBindIndexBuffer(cmd, index_pool.buffer, index_pool.offset, VK_INDEX_TYPE_UINT16);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice commands = s->command_slices[view];[m
[32m+[m[32m    BufferSlice count    = s->count_slices[view];[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_draw_indexed_indirect_count(s->vk, cmd, BYTE_SPAN(push), commands, count, SCENE_MAX_COMMANDS,[m
[32m+[m[32m                                    sizeof(SceneGpuDraw));[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    end_pass(cmd);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (s->hiz_levels) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        scene_hiz_build(s, cmd, depth, depth->bindless_index, 0);[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (uint32_t i = 1; i < s->hiz_levels; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            scene_hiz_build(s, cmd, &s->hiz[i - 1], s->hiz[i - 1].bindless_index, i);[m
[32m+[m[32m        for (uint32_t i = 0; i < s->hiz_levels; i++)[m
[32m+[m[32m            rt_transition_all(s->vk, cmd, &s->hiz[i], VK_IMAGE_LAYOUT_SHADER_READ_ONLY_OPTIMAL,[m
[32m+[m[32m                              VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_SHADER_SAMPLED_READ_BIT);[m
[32m+[m[32m        flush_barriers(s->vk, cmd);[m
[32m+[m[32m        if (s->hiz_warmup)[m
[32m+[m[32m            s->hiz_warmup--;[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_frame_begin(Scene *s, VkCommandBuffer cmd) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->instance_count          = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->view_count              = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->instance_upload_pending = false;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (s->hiz_disable_frames > 0)[m
[32m+[m[32m        s->hiz_disable_frames--;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    /* Frames in flight overlap: the previous frame's vertex/compute/indirect[m
[32m+[m[32m       reads of the count, palette, view, and instance buffers may still be[m
[32m+[m[32m       executing when this frame's transfer writes below begin. Submission[m
[32m+[m[32m       order adds no memory dependency, so fence every prior read (and prior[m
[32m+[m[32m       write) before this frame's first transfer write. dst=TRANSFER orders[m
[32m+[m[32m       all subsequent copies in this command buffer, not just these zeros. */[m
[32m+[m[32m    cmd_memory_barrier(cmd, VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_VERTEX_SHADER_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_COMPUTE_SHADER_BIT |[m
[32m+[m[32m                                 VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_DRAW_INDIRECT_BIT | VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        VK_ACCESS_2_MEMORY_READ_BIT | VK_ACCESS_2_MEMORY_WRITE_BIT,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        VK_PIPELINE_STAGE_2_TRANSFER_BIT, VK_ACCESS_2_TRANSFER_WRITE_BIT);[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    for (uint32_t i = 0; i < SCENE_MAX_VIEWS; i++) {[m
[32m+[m[32m        uint32_t zero = 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m        renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(s->vk, cmd, s->count_slices[i], (ByteSpan){&zero, sizeof(zero)});[m
[32m+[m[32m    }[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t czero[8] = {0};[m
[32m+[m[32m    renderer_upload_buffer_to_slice(s->vk, cmd, s->counter_slice, (ByteSpan){&czero, sizeof(czero)});[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_view_set(Scene *s, uint32_t view, const SceneViewDesc *desc) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (view >= SCENE_MAX_VIEWS)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!desc)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(s->views[view].rows, desc->rows, sizeof(desc->rows));[m
[32m+[m[32m    memcpy(s->views[view].sun, desc->sun, sizeof(desc->sun));[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].hiz_size[0]   = s->hiz_levels ? s->hiz[0].width : 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].hiz_size[1]   = s->hiz_levels ? s->hiz[0].height : 0;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].caps          = desc->caps;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].draws         = scene_slice_addr(s, s->command_slices[view]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].visible       = scene_slice_addr(s, s->visible_slices[view]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].instances     = scene_slice_addr(s, s->instance_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].mesh_sets     = scene_slice_addr(s, s->mesh_set_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].lod_table     = scene_slice_addr(s, s->lod_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].command_count = scene_slice_addr(s, s->count_slices[view]);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].counters      = scene_slice_addr(s, s->counter_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].materials     = scene_slice_addr(s, s->material_slice);[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].sampler       = s->sampler;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].lod_target    = s->lod_target;[m
[32m+[m[32m    s->views[view].draw_count    = 0;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (view >= s->view_count)[m
[32m+[m[32m        s->view_count = view + 1;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid scene_debug_dump(Scene *s) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t       slot = (s->vk->current_frame + MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT - 2) % MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const uint8_t *raw  = (const uint8_t *)s->readback[slot].mapping;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!raw)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const uint32_t *c    = (const uint32_t *)(raw + 0);[m
[32m+[m[32m    const uint32_t *cmd0 = (const uint32_t *)(raw + 32);[m
[32m+[m[32m    const uint32_t *cmd1 = (const uint32_t *)(raw + 64);[m
[32m+[m[32m    const uint32_t *vis  = (const uint32_t *)(raw + 96);[m
[32m+[m[32m    fprintf(stderr,[m
[32m+[m[32m            "[cull] frustum=%u hiz=%u dropped=%u drawn=%u | cmd0={idx=%u inst=%u first=%u voff=%u base=%u id=%u} "[m
[32m+[m[32m            "cmd1={idx=%u inst=%u first=%u} vis0=%08x vis1=%08x vis2=%08x vis3=%08x\n",[m
[32m+[m[32m            c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3], cmd0[0], cmd0[1], cmd0[2], cmd0[3], cmd0[4], cmd0[5], cmd1[0], cmd1[1], cmd1[2],[m
[32m+[m[32m            vis[0], vis[1], vis[2], vis[3]);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32mbool scene_counters_read(Scene *s, SceneCounters *out) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!out)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    uint32_t        slot = (s->vk->current_frame + MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT - 2) % MAX_FRAMES_IN_FLIGHT;[m
[32m+[m[32m    const uint32_t *raw  = (const uint32_t *)s->readback[slot].mapping;[m
[32m+[m[32m    if (!raw)[m
[32m+[m[32m        return false;[m
[32m+[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->submitted        = s->last_submitted;[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->culled_frustum   = raw[0];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->culled_hiz       = raw[1];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->dropped_commands = raw[2];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->drawn            = raw[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->commands         = raw[3];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->lod[0]           = raw[4];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->lod[1]           = raw[5];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->lod[2]           = raw[6];[m
[32m+[m[32m    out->lod[3]           = raw[7];[m
[32m+[m[32m    return true;[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m

[32m+[m[32m// Queue submission order creates no memory dependency between frames in flight.[m
[32m+[m[32m// Prior frames' shader/indirect reads must execute before this frame's transfer[m
[32m+[m[32m// writes into the same persistent buffers, or the writes land mid-read.[m
[32m+[m[32mvoid cmd_memory_barrier(VkCommandBuffer cmd, VkPipelineStageFlags2 src_stage, VkAccessFlags2 src_access,[m
[32m+[m[32m                        VkPipelineStageFlags2 dst_stage, VkAccessFlags2 dst_access) {[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkMemoryBarrier2 barrier = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        .sType       = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_MEMORY_BARRIER_2,[m
[32m+[m[32m        .srcStageMask = src_stage,[m
[32m+[m[32m        .srcAccessMask = src_access,[m
[32m+[m[32m        .dstStageMask = dst_stage,[m
[32m+[m[32m        .dstAccessMask = dst_access,[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m[32m    VkDependencyInfo dep_info = {[m
[32m+[m[32m        .sType           = VK_STRUCTURE_TYPE_DEPENDENCY_INFO,[m
[32m+[m[32m        .memoryBarrierCount = 1,[m
[32m+[m[32m        .pMemoryBarriers    = &barrier,[m
[32m+[m[32m    };[m
[32m+[m[32m    vkCmdPipelineBarrier2(cmd, &dep_info);[m
[32m+[m[32m}[m
